gui = script.Parent.Parent.Parent --the Shop frame
tmi = gui.TooManyItems
enabled = gui.Enabled
player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
itempurchase = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ItemBuyRequest

input = game:GetService("UserInputService")
touch = false
taps = 0
if input.TouchEnabled == true then
	touch = true
	gui.TouchNotice.Visible = true
end

function onMouseEnter()
	local desc = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemDesc")
	local cost = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemCost")
	if desc ~= nil and cost ~= nil then
		gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = desc.Value
		gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..cost.Value
		gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = script.Parent.Name
	end
end

function onMouseButton1Click() --clone the item into their backpack
	itemsbought = gui.ItemsBought
	if touch then
		local desc = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemDesc")
		local cost = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemCost")
		if desc ~= nil and cost ~= nil then
			gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = desc.Value
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..cost.Value
			gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = script.Parent.Name
		end
		taps = taps + 1
		if taps == 2 then
			taps = 0
			if enabled.Value == true then
			enabled.Value = false
			if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) < 25 then
				if player:findFirstChild("Backpack") ~= nil then
					tools = player.Backpack:GetChildren()
					if #tools < 10 then
						
							if itemsbought.Value < 6 then
								local purchase = itempurchase:InvokeServer(gui.Parent.Parent.Name,script.Parent.Name)
								if purchase then
									itemsbought.Value = itemsbought.Value + 1
								else
									tmi.Text = "You do not have enough cash! You can earn some cash over time, or purchase more in the Game Pass Shop."
									tmi.Visible = true
									wait(4)
									tmi.Visible = false
								end
							else
								tmi.Text = "You are buying a lot of items - please wait and give the other customers a chance!"
								tmi.Visible = true
								wait(6)
								tmi.Visible = false
								gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
							end
							
						
					else
						tmi.Text = "You have too many items! Use Backspace to drop unwanted items."
						tmi.Visible = true
						wait(4)
						tmi.Visible = false
					end
				end
			else
				tmi.Text = "You have walked too far away from the shopkeeper!"
				tmi.Visible = true
				wait(4)
				tmi.Visible = false
				if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) >= 25 then
					gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
				end
			end
			wait(1)
			enabled.Value = true
		end
		end
		wait(.5)
		if taps > 0 then
			taps = taps - 1
		end
	else
		if enabled.Value == true then
			enabled.Value = false
			if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) < 25 then
				if player:findFirstChild("Backpack") ~= nil then
					tools = player.Backpack:GetChildren()
					if #tools < 10 then
						
							if itemsbought.Value < 6 then
								local purchase = itempurchase:InvokeServer(gui.Parent.Parent.Name,script.Parent.Name)
								if purchase then
									itemsbought.Value = itemsbought.Value + 1
								else
									tmi.Text = "You do not have enough cash! You can earn some cash over time, or purchase more in the Game Pass Shop."
									tmi.Visible = true
									wait(4)
									tmi.Visible = false
								end
							else
								tmi.Text = "You are buying a lot of items - please wait and give the other customers a chance!"
								tmi.Visible = true
								wait(6)
								tmi.Visible = false
								gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
							end
							
						
					else
						tmi.Text = "You have too many items! Use Backspace to drop unwanted items."
						tmi.Visible = true
						wait(4)
						tmi.Visible = false
					end
				end
			else
				tmi.Text = "You have walked too far away from the shopkeeper!"
				tmi.Visible = true
				wait(4)
				tmi.Visible = false
				if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) >= 25 then
					gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
				end
			end
			wait(1)
			enabled.Value = true
		end

	end
end

script.Parent.MouseEnter:connect(onMouseEnter)
script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:connect(onMouseButton1Click)V
=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=95573 runSha256=13c267cf91db4bf12567ef5b63dc31d12e7609a0a1ca0ed3364fd189372bd4ab ===
spraysize = 5
cooldown = 1.9
baseid = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendSprayPaint.OnClientEvent:connect(function(plr,sprayid, mousepos, surface, target)
	if target == nil then return end
	local canspray=false
	if (surface==Enum.NormalId.Front or surface==Enum.NormalId.Back) and target.Size.x>=spraysize and target.Size.y>=spraysize then
		canspray=true
	elseif (surface==Enum.NormalId.Left or surface==Enum.NormalId.Right) and target.Size.y>=spraysize and target.Size.z>=spraysize then
		canspray=true
	elseif (surface==Enum.NormalId.Top or surface==Enum.NormalId.Bottom) and target.Size.x>=spraysize and target.Size.z>=spraysize then
		canspray=true
	end
	if canspray then
		local p=Instance.new("Part")
		local d=Instance.new("Decal")
		d.Texture=baseid..tostring(sprayid-1)
		d.Face=surface
		d.Parent=p
		p.Name="!SPRAY_"..plr.Name
		p.formFactor="Custom"
		p.Anchored=false
		p.CanCollide=false
		p.Transparency=1
		p.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.01,1,1)
		if surface==Enum.NormalId.Front or surface==Enum.NormalId.Back then
			p.Size=Vector3.new(spraysize,spraysize,.2)
		elseif surface==Enum.NormalId.Left or surface==Enum.NormalId.Right then
			p.Size=Vector3.new(.2,spraysize,spraysize)
		elseif surface==Enum.NormalId.Top or surface==Enum.NormalId.Bottom then
			p.Size=Vector3.new(spraysize,.2,spraysize)
		end
		local w=Instance.new("Weld")
		w.Part0=target
		w.Part1=p
		local cf=CFrame.new(target.CFrame:pointToObjectSpace(mousepos))
		w.C0=cf
		w.C1=CFrame.new(0,0,0)
		w.Parent=target
		p.CFrame=target.CFrame:toWorldSpace(cf)		--this is here to position the spray other wise it will be at 0,0,0 if the target is anchored
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p,60)
		p.Parent=game.Workspace
	end
end)
=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=99396 runSha256=c2e643a878ffd292e0dfe6a2293ddf0a44ec70defae342a580bcdfd96f0e19e8 ===
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local gui = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local container = gui:WaitForChild("ContextActionsGui")

if plr.Character == nil or plr.Character.Parent == nil then
	plr.CharacterAdded:wait()
end

local char = plr.Character
local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local playerstatus = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId):WaitForChild("SpecialStatus")

local CurrentObject = nil
local actions = {}

local hover_icon = "rbxassetid://231195429"
local normal_icon = "rbxasset://textures/ArrowFarCursor.png"
local select_sound = "rbxassetid://231188062"

--[[

on RenderStepped: 

IF NO TOOL EQUIPPED:


IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- get mouse Hit. if the object's ContextActionsModelName is the same as the CurrentObjectHoveringOver, change nothing.
- if ContextActionsModelName is different (or there is currently a CurrentObjectHoveringOver, and a new ContextActionsModelName is not found), then remove the "Interact with" overlay if there is one and set mouse icon to White. if there is a ContextActionsModelName in the new object, make that the overlay and set mouse icon to Green.

- every frame of this, update any "Interact with" overlay's position


IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is outside of context menu's range, remove context menu




otherwise, IF TOOL EQUIPPED: 
- remove any overlays, do not change mouse icon







on MouseButton1Down:
IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is over object, generate the object's action menu. set mouse icon to White

IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is not over context menu, remove context menu

each action button has its own Clicked connection which will run the action's module and then remove the context menu

]]

function isOffScreen(point,bx,by) --bx and by are part of AbsoluteSize
	if point.x < 0 or point.x > bx or point.y < 0 or point.y > by then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function checkOffScreenCorners(element) --corners are like quadrants
	local pos = element.Position
	local size = element.Size
	local c1 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c2 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c3 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local c4 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local bx = container.AbsoluteSize.x
	local by = container.AbsoluteSize.y
	return isOffScreen(c1,bx,by),isOffScreen(c2,bx,by), isOffScreen(c3,bx,by),isOffScreen(c4,bx,by)
end

function handsAreFree()
	if plr.Character then
		for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("HopperBin") then
				return false
			end
		end
		for i,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
				return false
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

function getAvailableActions(obj)
	local getactions = {}
	local objname = obj:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
	if objname then
		objname = objname.Value
		local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if objdata then
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("StringValue") then
					table.insert(getactions,v)
				end
			end
			
			local owner = obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
			if owner then
				if owner.Value == plr.Name then
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("StringValue") then
							table.insert(getactions,v)
						end
					end
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
						if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then --if this status name is inside the player's special statuses
							for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do --add any actions that are under that category
								table.insert(getactions,x)
							end
						end
					end
				else
					if owner.Value == "" then
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.NoOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					else
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.OtherOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			for i,v in pairs(objdata.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata.NotSpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if not playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			
		end
	end
	actions = getactions
end

gui:WaitForChild("ActionMenuSelection").Event:connect(function(num)
	if CurrentObject ~= nil then
		local objname = CurrentObject:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
		if objname then
			objname = objname.Value
			local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
			if objdata then
				local action = objdata:FindFirstChild(num,true)
				if action then
					local module = require(action.ModuleScript)
					module.RunAction(plr,CurrentObject) --run the action now!
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

local RMBdown = false

function WithinValidDistance(hit,target)
	local model = target.Parent
	local maxdistance = 32
	if model:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsDistance") then
		maxdistance = model.ContextActionsDistance.Value
	end
	if char and hrp and (hrp.Position - hit.p).magnitude < maxdistance then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function MouseOverActiveFrame(x,y)
	local isover = false
	local function recurse(instance)
		for _,v in pairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
			if (v:IsA("Frame") or v:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) and v.Visible == true then
				if (x > v.AbsolutePosition.x and x < v.AbsolutePosition.x + v.AbsoluteSize.x and y > v.AbsolutePosition.y and y < v.AbsolutePosition.y + v.AbsoluteSize.y) then
					isover = true
					break
				else
					recurse(v)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if gui:FindFirstChild("MainMenu") then
		recurse(gui.MainMenu)
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("PhoneGUI") and gui.PhoneGUI.Frame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.PhoneGUI)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("HouseEditMode") and gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
	local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
	local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
	if handsAreFree() then
		if not actionmenu then --manage interact overlay if there's no action menu
			local targetmodel
			if mouse.Target then
				targetmodel = (mouse.Target.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName") or mouse.Target.Parent:IsA("Accoutrement") and mouse.Target.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName"))
			end
			if targetmodel then
				targetmodel = targetmodel.Parent
			end
			if mouse.Target and targetmodel and not RMBdown and WithinValidDistance(mouse.Hit,mouse.Target) and not MouseOverActiveFrame(mouse.X,mouse.Y) then
				if targetmodel ~= CurrentObject then --if object is valid and it is not the same one being hovered over already, get actions
					getAvailableActions(targetmodel)
					if #actions > 0 then
						CurrentObject = targetmodel
						if overlay then
							overlay:Destroy()
						end
						mouse.Icon = hover_icon
						overlay = container.BaseInteractOverlay:clone()
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Name = "InteractOverlay"
						if #actions == 1 then
							overlay.Text = actions[1].Value
						else
							local modelname = targetmodel.ContextActionsModelName.Value
							if modelname == "Player" then
								modelname = targetmodel.Name
							end
							overlay.Text = "Interact with " ..modelname
						end
						overlay.Parent = container
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Size = UDim2.new(0,overlay.TextBounds.X + 15,0,16)
					end
				end
			else --remove overlay if not hovering over valid object - either no CAMN, too far away, hovering over active GUI frame, or RMB is down
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				CurrentObject = nil
				if overlay then
					overlay:Destroy()
				end
			end
			if overlay then
				overlay.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y - overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(overlay)
				if c4 == true and c1 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position - UDim2.new(0,overlay.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
				end
				if c1 == true and c2 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				end
			end
		else
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset - 40 or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset + 40 or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset - 40 or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset + 40 then
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	else
		if actionmenu then
			actionmenu:Destroy()
		end
		if overlay then
			overlay:Destroy()
		end
		CurrentObject = nil
	end
end)

mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if not actionmenu then 
			
			if CurrentObject ~= nil and #actions > 0 then --create an action menu for the object, then set mouse icon
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",gui)
				sound.SoundId = select_sound
				sound:Play()
				sound.TimePosition = .05
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.5)
				if #actions >= 2 then
					mouse.Icon = normal_icon
					local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
					if overlay then
						overlay:Destroy()
					end
					actionmenu = container.BaseActionMenu:clone()
					actionmenu.Name = "ActionMenu"
					actionmenu.Parent = container
					actionmenu.Visible = true
					local maxwidth = 0
					for i,v in pairs(actions) do
						local button = actionmenu.BaseSelector:clone()
						button.Name = v.Name
						button.Text = " "..v.Value
						button.Parent = actionmenu
						button.Visible = true
						button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0, (#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 2) * 20)
						if button.TextBounds.X > maxwidth then
							maxwidth = button.TextBounds.X
						end
						
						if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") and i == 1 then
							game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = button
						end
					end
					actionmenu.Size = UDim2.new(0,maxwidth + 3,0,(#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 1) * 20)
					actionmenu.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y)
					local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(actionmenu)
					if c1 == true and c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,0,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c1 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
					end
				else
					gui.ActionMenuSelection:Fire(actions[1].Name) --only one action
				end
			end
			
		else --action menu already open - is mouse in range of it?
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Down:connect(function() --remove on right click outside of menu
	RMBdown = true
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if actionmenu then 
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Up:connect(function()
	RMBdown = false
end)

if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	
	UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(input,gameprocessed)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect then
				local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
				if actionmenu and actionmenu.Parent ~= nil then
					actionmenu:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

	if property == "SeatPart" and totaltime.Value < 120 and hum.SeatPart ~= nil and hum.SeatPart.Name == "Drive" then
		local car = hum.SeatPart.Parent
		if car:FindFirstChild("DriverLocked") and car.DriverLocked.Value == true and car.Owner.Value ~= plr.Name then
			notification:Fire("You can't drive this person's car. Spawn your own car via the Cars menu!")
		end
	end
end)

			end
		end
	end

		end
	end

			menu.Visible = false
			button.BackgroundColor3 = defaultcolor
			return
		end
		
		menu.Visible = true

				if v ~= button and v.Name ~= "ShopButton" and v.Name ~= "MobileAd" and v.Name ~= "ToggleVisibility" then --make other buttons def.

				end

				if v.Name:match("menu_") then
					if v.Name ~= "menu_"..button.Name then --make frame invis
						v.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end
		
	end

			end

		end

function GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 0 then
		return "Ends now!"
	end
	
	local d, h, m, s 
	
	d = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
	if d == 0 then
		d = ""
	else
		d = d..":"
	end
	
	h = math.floor((timeleft / 3600) % 24)
	if h < 10 then
		if h == 0 then
			if d == "" then
				h = ""
			else
				h = "00:"
			end
		else
			if d == "" then
				h = h..":"
			else
				h = "0"..h..":"
			end
		end
	else
		h = h..":"
	end
	
	m = math.floor((timeleft / 60) % 60)
	if m < 10 then
		if m == 0 then
			if h == "" then
				m = "0:"
			else
				m = "00:"
			end
		else
			if h == "" then
				m = m..":"
			else
				m = "0"..m..":"
			end
		end
	else
		m = m..":"
	end
	
	s = timeleft % 60
	if s < 10 then
		s = "0"..s
	end
	
	return d..h..m..s
end

function SetTimeString()
	local timeleft = (jointime + 86400 * 7) - currenttimeobj.Value
	if timeleft <= 0 then
		timelabel.Text = "Offer about to expire!"
	else
		timelabel.Text = "Offer ends in "..GetTimeString(timeleft)
	end
end

SetTimeString()

currenttimeobj.Changed:connect(function()
	SetTimeString()
end)



local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

-- RHKMan!! --

-- Have a handle to the game service in case we need to get the time object
r = game:service("RunService")

uses = 0

-- Tool and the Object, in this case RHKMan
local Tool = script.Parent

-- Just a partial lock, which disallows multiple instances of the onActivated function
Tool.Enabled = true

-- Initialize the Animation variable
local RHKAnim = nil

-- Damage!!
local RHK_damage = 2

-- To Connect with our blow function
local connection = nil

-- RHK Sound File
local RHKS = Instance.new("Sound")
RHKS.SoundId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=45763159"
RHKS.Parent = Tool.Handle
RHKS.Volume = .5

--Blow function, called when the character touches another character
function blow(hit)
	if cantHitNow then return end
   -- find, the humanoid object of the character that got hit
	local humanoid = hit.Parent:findFirstChild("Humanoid")
	-- Find the our tool's character
	local vCharacter = Tool.Parent
	-- Get a handle to our player from the game space
	local vPlayer = game.Players:playerFromCharacter(vCharacter)
	-- Get the humanoid object our the player character
	local hum = vCharacter:findFirstChild("Humanoid") 
	-- if the humanoid object is not nil and is not the same as the hit character
	if humanoid~= nil and humanoid ~= hum and hum ~= nil then
		-- Get the handle to the left leg
		local left_leg = vCharacter:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")	 or vCharacter:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")		
		if(left_leg ~= nil) then			
			-- If the left leg of the player tocuhes the humanoid then tag him			
			tagHumanoid(humanoid, vPlayer)
			-- Do the @#$% damage to him
			humanoid:TakeDamage(RHK_damage)
			if connection then connection:disconnect() connection = nil end
			wait(1.0)
			-- and untag him after a second so that the damage isn't applied continously
			untagHumanoid(humanoid)
		end
	end
end

-- Tagging and Untagging are done for leaderboards to find the source of the incoming damage
-- Function, to tag the player to the humanoid object
function tagHumanoid(humanoid, player)
	local creator_tag = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
	-- Creates a value for the player and makes the humanoid its parent
	creator_tag.Value = player
	creator_tag.Name = "creator"
	creator_tag.Parent = humanoid
end

-- Function, to untag the player from the humanoid
function untagHumanoid(humanoid)
	if humanoid ~= nil then
		-- Check if the creator tag is enabled
		local tag = humanoid:findFirstChild("creator")
		if tag ~= nil then
		-- if so, nullify the tag
			tag.Parent = nil
		end
	end
end
-- Actions to do when an item is activated
function onActivated()
	-- Make the Ninja potion transparent
	Tool.Handle.Transparency = 1
	-- Tentative lock, to disable multiple instances of this function
	if not Tool.Enabled then 
		return
	end
-- Check if the handle to the Player is still available in the space
	local MyPlayer = Tool.Parent
	if MyPlayer == nil then 
		return 
	end	

	-- Get a handle to the Torso of our character to rotate it
	local MyTorso = Tool.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if MyTorso == nil then
		return
	end

	-- Check to see if the humanoid is still in the world
	local  MyHumanoid= MyPlayer:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	if MyHumanoid == nil then 
		return
	end

	-- Get a handle to the left leg of our character to check for hit
	local MyLeftLeg = Tool.Parent:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") or Tool.Parent:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
	if MyLeftLeg == nil then
		return
	end
	
	script.Parent.AddUse:FireServer()

	Tool.Enabled = false

	-- Load the Animation
	RHKAnim = MyHumanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.RHK2)	
	-- Play Animation
	RHKAnim:Play()	
	
	-- Here, we are creating different frames of reference for our character's
	-- torso, so that we can rotate him 360 degrees. 6 steps of 60 deg each 
	torsoFrame1 = MyTorso.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 1*math.pi/3, 0)
	torsoFrame2 = MyTorso.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 2*math.pi/3, 0)
	torsoFrame3 = MyTorso.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0)
	torsoFrame4 = MyTorso.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 4*math.pi/3, 0)
	torsoFrame5 = MyTorso.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 5*math.pi/3, 0)
	torsoFrame6 = MyTorso.CFrame

	-- Applying a force in the direction that we require on the body
	bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	bodyGyro.P = 100000
	bodyGyro.D = 3000
	bodyGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(bodyGyro.P, bodyGyro.P, bodyGyro.P)
	bodyGyro.cframe = torsoFrame1
	bodyGyro.Parent = MyTorso

	-- The Animation lasts for a total of 0.8 seconds
	-- Match the Torso rotation with the animation
	wait(.4/3)
	bodyGyro.cframe = torsoFrame2
	wait(.4/3)
	-- Make the character jump for the kick
	MyHumanoid.Jump = true
	-- Check for the blow hit only after the character has jumped
	connection = MyLeftLeg.Touched:connect(blow)

	-- play the sound
	RHKS:Play()
	bodyGyro.cframe = torsoFrame3	
	wait(.4/3)
	bodyGyro.cframe = torsoFrame4
	wait(.4/3)
	bodyGyro.cframe = torsoFrame5
	wait(.4/3)
	bodyGyro.cframe = torsoFrame6
	wait(.4/3)

	wait(.5)
	-- Once the animation is played, disable the connection so that damage isn't
	-- applied for normal left leg collisions
	if connection then connection:disconnect() connection = nil end
	bodyGyro:Destroy()
	wait(.5)
	
	script.Parent.CheckUses:InvokeServer()

	Tool.Enabled = true	
	
	-- Bring back the Ninja Potion!
	Tool.Handle.Transparency = 0
end

-- On Equip re-enable our sound
function onEquipped()
	RHKS.Volume = .5
end

-- On Unequip, stop the animation, remove the sound and disable the connection
function onUnequipped()	
	if RHKAnim ~= nil then RHKAnim:Stop() end
	if connection then connection:disconnect() connection = nil end	
	if RHKS ~= nil then RHKS:Stop() RHKS.Volume = 0.0 end
end
	
-- Connect our function to the Tool's Activate and other interfaces
Tool.Activated:connect(onActivated)
Tool.Equipped:connect(onEquipped)
Tool.Unequipped:connect(onUnequipped)

	end

	if otherperson == true then
		sendername = thread.Name
	end
	
	if type(msg) ~= "string" then
		sendername = msg.Name
		msg = msg.Value
	end
	
	local sender = game.Players:FindFirstChild(sendername)
	if sender == nil then return end
	
	local newtext = threadview.Messages.BaseButton:clone()
	newtext.Name = sendername
	newtext.Position = newtext.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,offset)
	if newtext.Name == plr.Name then
		newtext.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.9,.9,.9)
	else
		newtext.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,1,1)
	end
	newtext.Text = sendername..": "..msg
	newtext.Parent = threadview.Messages
	if newtext.TextFits == false and not newtext.Text:match("%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w%w") and not newtext.Text:match("mmmmmmm") then
		local iv = 0
		repeat
			newtext.Size = newtext.Size + UDim2.new(0,0,0,14)
			iv = iv + 1
		until newtext.TextFits == true or iv == 6
	end
	threadview.Messages.CanvasSize = threadview.Messages.CanvasSize + UDim2.new(0,0,0,(newtext.Size.Y.Offset + 1))
	newtext.Visible = true
	--for i=1,10 do
	threadview.Messages.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0,(threadview.Messages.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - 183))
	--wait()
	--end
	
	offset = 0
end

local module = {}

module.canvases = {}

local msgcooldown = {}

local maxpaint = 250

local isevent = true --turn this off after event

function AddToCooldown(plr)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		table.insert(msgcooldown,plr.Name)
		wait(3)
		table.remove(msgcooldown,1)
	end))
end

module.SetCanvases = function()
	local tempcanvases = {}
	for _,v in pairs(workspace.CanvasPapers:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == "!Canvas" then
			table.insert(tempcanvases,v)
		end
		module.canvases = tempcanvases
	end
end

function CheckCooldown(plr)
	for _,v in pairs(msgcooldown) do
		if plr.Name == v then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

function Notification(plr,msg)
	if CheckCooldown(plr) then
		if game:GetService("RunService"):IsServer() then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, msg)
		else
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire(msg)
		end
		AddToCooldown(plr)
	end
end

--[[module.CheckForEventWin = function(plr)
	if isevent then
		local paintmodel = workspace.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name)
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		if paintmodel and data and data.PaintBadge.Value == false then
			local children = #paintmodel:GetChildren()
			if children >= 50 then
				data.PaintBadge.Value = true
				coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
					game:GetService("BadgeService"):AwardBadge(plr.userId, game.ReplicatedStorage.PaintingBadgeID.Value)
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.EventPrizeAwardedToPlayer:FireClient(plr, "Painting")
				end))
			end
		end
	end
end]]

function ApplyPaint(plr, paintmodel, canvas, position, mode, brickcolor)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name = "ServerPart"
	if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
		part.Name = "LocalPart"
	end
	part.Shape = "Ball"
	part.Size = Vector3.new(.3,.3,.3)
	part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
	part.Anchored = true
	
	if mode == "Paint" then
		part.BrickColor = brickcolor or BrickColor.Red()
		part.TopSurface = "Smooth"
		part.BottomSurface = "Smooth"
		part.Material = "SmoothPlastic"
	else
		part.Transparency = 1
	end
	
	if mode == "Erase" then
		for _,v in pairs(paintmodel:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Part") then
				v.CanCollide = true
			end
		end
	end
	
	part.Parent = paintmodel
	
	local touchingparts = part:GetTouchingParts()
	
	part.Parent = paintmodel
	
	if mode == "Erase" then
		for _,v in pairs(paintmodel:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Part") then
				v.CanCollide = false
			end
		end
	end
	
	part.CanCollide = false
	
	if mode == "Paint" then
		local istouching = false
		for _,v in pairs(touchingparts) do
			if v == canvas then
				istouching = true
			end
		end
		if not istouching then part:Destroy() return end
		
		if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlacePaintRequest:InvokeServer(canvas, position, mode, brickcolor)
			if part then
				part:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		return true
	elseif mode == "Erase" then
		for _,v in pairs(touchingparts) do
			if v.Parent == paintmodel then
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		part:Destroy()
		
		if #paintmodel:GetChildren() == 0 then
			canvas.CanvasOwner.Value = nil
		end
		
		if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlacePaintRequest:InvokeServer(canvas, position, mode)
		end
		
		return true
	end
	
end

module.GetMaxPaint = function()
	return maxpaint
end

module.EraseAllPaint = function(plr)
	local paintmodel = game.ReplicatedStorage.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) or workspace.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name)
	--[[if game:GetService("RunService"):IsServer() then
		module.CheckForEventWin(plr)
	end]]
	if paintmodel then
		for _,v in pairs(paintmodel:GetChildren()) do
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for _,v in pairs(module.canvases) do
		if v.CanvasOwner.Value == plr then
			v.CanvasOwner.Value = nil
		end
	end
	if game:GetService("RunService"):IsClient() then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.EraseAllPaint:FireServer()
	end
end

module.PlacePaint = function(plr, canvas, position, mode, brickcolor)
	local paintmodel = game.ReplicatedStorage.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) or workspace.ArtCanvases:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) --holds all of the player's paint parts
	if paintmodel and canvas and position and mode then
		local owner = canvas.CanvasOwner.Value
		if owner ~= nil and owner ~= plr then
			Notification(plr, "Another player has claimed this canvas. Try painting on a different canvas!")
			return
		end
		for _,v in pairs(module.canvases) do
			if v ~= canvas and v.CanvasOwner.Value == plr then
				Notification(plr, "You are already painting on another canvas. Erase the paint from your old canvas first.")
				return
			end
		end
		if owner == nil then
			canvas.CanvasOwner.Value = plr
		end
			
		local paintcount = #paintmodel:GetChildren()
		if mode == "Paint" and paintcount >= maxpaint then
			Notification(plr, "You've used up all of your available paint. Erase some parts if you wish to continue painting more!")
			return
		end
		--[[if game:GetService("RunService"):IsServer() and mode == "Paint" and paintcount > 175 then
			module.CheckForEventWin(plr)
		end]]
		
		coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(ApplyPaint),plr,paintmodel,canvas,position,mode,brickcolor)
		--ApplyPaint(plr,paintmodel,canvas,position,mode,brickcolor)

	end
end

return module

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleCarLock.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,car)
	if car and car.Name == "vehicle_"..plr.Name then
		local lock = car.SeatsLocked
		if lock.Value == true then
			lock.Value = false
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarUnlocked")
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarLocked")
		else
			lock.Value = true
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarLocked")
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarUnlocked")
		end
	end
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SpawnCarRequest.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,position)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data and CheckCooldown(plr) and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - position.p).magnitude < 52 then
		coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(AddToCooldown),plr)
		for i,v in pairs(game.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "vehicle_"..plr.Name then
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarLocked")
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"DriverUnlocked")
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"CarUnlocked")
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"DriverLocked")
		
		local findcar = getItemDataFromID(plr.SelectedCar.Value)
		local paint = getItemDataFromID(plr.SelectedPaint.Value)
		
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(plr.SelectedCar.Value) == nil or (paint ~= nil and paint.Name:match("paint_") and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(plr.SelectedPaint.Value) == nil) then print("car spawn fail "..plr.Name) return end --bad!
		
		if findcar then
			
			local boxsize,boxcframe = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.GetBoundingBox)(findcar)
			local bb = Instance.new("Part")
			bb.Transparency = 1
			bb.Anchored = true
			bb.Size = boxsize
			bb.Parent = workspace
			bb.CFrame = CFrame.new(position.p + Vector3.new(0,boxsize.y / 2,0))
			local parts = bb:GetTouchingParts()
			bb:Destroy()
			for _,v in pairs(parts) do
				if v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or (v.Parent:FindFirstChild("DriverLocked") and v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Owner") and v.Parent.Owner.Value ~= plr.Name) then --too close to something else
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"You cannot spawn your car here, as it is too close to yourself or another car/person.")
					return
				end
			end
			
			local car = findcar:clone()
			car.Name = "vehicle_"..plr.Name
			
			local owner = car:FindFirstChild("Owner")
			if not owner then
				owner = Instance.new("StringValue")
				owner.Name = "Owner"
			end
			owner.Value = plr.Name
			owner.Parent = car
			
			local ca = Instance.new("StringValue")
			ca.Name = "ContextActionsModelName"
			ca.Value = "Vehicle"
			ca.Parent = car
			
			if paint then
				for i,v in pairs(car:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Name == "Paintable" then
						v.BrickColor = paint.BrickColorValue.Value
						v.Reflectance = paint.Reflectance.Value
						v.Transparency = paint.Transparency.Value
					end
					local x = v:FindFirstChild("Sparkles")
					if x then
						x.SparkleColor = paint.BrickColorValue.Value.Color
					end
					local y = v:FindFirstChild("SpecialLight")
					if y then
						y.Color = paint.BrickColorValue.Value.Color
					end
				end
			end
			car.DetectLeave.Disabled = false
			
			local engine = car:FindFirstChild("Engine") or car:FindFirstChild("Anchor")
			car.PrimaryPart = engine
			for i,v in pairs(car:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Part") and v.Shape == Enum.PartType.Cylinder then
					local weld = Instance.new("Weld") 
					weld.Part0 = engine
					weld.Part1 = v 
					weld.C0 = engine.CFrame:inverse() 
					weld.C1 = v.CFrame:inverse() 
					weld.Parent = v
				end
			end
			car.Parent = game.Workspace
			car:MakeJoints()
			car:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(position.p + Vector3.new(0,6,0)))
			--car.Engine.CFrame = CFrame.new(position.p + Vector3.new(0,8,0))
			wait(.25)
			for i,v in pairs(car:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Part") and v.Shape == Enum.PartType.Cylinder and v:FindFirstChild("Weld") then
					v.Weld:Destroy()
				end
			end
			return true
		end
	end
end

--[[

EACH MESSAGE WILL CONTAIN THIS DATA:
[os.time()] = {

["sender"] = "Sender name"
["msg"] = "Message text"
["local"] = bool
["sendto"] = {} --table of players near the given position. if local == true, the message will only be globally 
distibuted to players on this list. if local == false, then this list only applies to local chat. (it is a global message)

}

]]

local chat = game:GetService("Chat")

function FormUniqueMessageData(msgdata) --make a new table cuz we can't mess w/ the original one
	local datatosend = {}
	for i,v in pairs(msgdata) do
		datatosend[i] = v
	end
	return datatosend
end

function distributeMessage(plr,msgdata,sender)
	if plr.Parent ~= nil then
		local canchatwithuser
		pcall(function()
			canchatwithuser = game:GetService("Chat"):CanUsersChatAsync(sender.userId, plr.userId)
		end)
		if not canchatwithuser then return end
		local datatosend = FormUniqueMessageData(msgdata)
		local mode = plr:FindFirstChild("localchat")
		if sender and mode and mode:FindFirstChild(sender.Name) then return end
		if plr ~= sender and sender ~= nil then
			local filteredstring
			local success,reason = pcall(function()
				filteredstring = chat:FilterStringAsync(msgdata["msg"],sender,plr)
			end)
			if success ~= true and reason ~= nil then
				print("Failed to filter message from "..sender.Name.." to "..plr.Name..": "..reason)
				return
			end
			if filteredstring == nil then filteredstring = msgdata["msg"] end
			datatosend["msg"] = filteredstring
		end
		if plr ~= sender then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.DistributeMessage:FireClient(plr,datatosend)
		end
	end
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendGameChatMessage.Event:connect(function(target,msg)
	if target == "all" then
		for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendGameChatMessage:Fire(v,msg)
		end
	end
	local msgdata = {
		
		["sender"] = "Server Message",
		["msg"] = msg,
		["local"] = false,
		["sendto"] = {target},
		["chatcolor"] = Color3.new(1,0,0),
		["teamcolor"] = Color3.new(1,0,0),
		["special"] = nil,
		["vip"] = false,
		
	}
	distributeMessage(target,msgdata,nil)
end)

end

	for _,v in pairs(skatecooldown) do
		if v == plr.Name then
			return false
		end
	end
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(AddToSkateCooldown),plr)
	return true
end

		local msg = rawmsg
		
		local sender = plr.Name
		local color = plr:FindFirstChild("chatcolor")
		if color then
			color = color.Value
		else
			color = Color3.new(0,0,0)
		end
		
		local sendto = {}
		
		if plr.Character then
			local torso = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if torso then
				for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - torso.Position).magnitude < 76 then
						table.insert(sendto,v)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		

		local msgdata = {
			
			["sender"] = sender,
			["msg"] = msg,
			["local"] = chatsetting.Value,
			["sendto"] = sendto,
			["chatcolor"] = color,
			["teamcolor"] = plr.TeamColor.Color,
			["special"] = messagetype,
			["vip"] = game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(plr,"VIP"),
			
		}
		
		
		if chatsetting.Value == false then
			for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				distributeMessage(v,msgdata,plr)
			end
		else
			for i,v in pairs(sendto) do
				distributeMessage(v,msgdata,plr)
			end
		end
		
	elseif plr:FindFirstChild("CHATMUTE") and string.sub(rawmsg,1,2) ~= "/e" then
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendGameChatMessage:Fire(plr,"You are currently muted.")
	end
end

game.Players.ChildAdded:connect(function(plr)
	
	local chatsetting = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	chatsetting.Name = "localchat"
	chatsetting.Value = false
	chatsetting.Parent = plr
	
	local isadmin = false
	
	for i,v in pairs(_G["admins"]) do
		if plr.Name == v then
			isadmin = true
		end
	end
	
	plr.Chatted:connect(function(rawmsg)
		HandleChatted(plr,rawmsg,chatsetting,isadmin)
	end)
	
	--handle the hoverboard case here... cuz why not???
	local bp = plr:WaitForChild("Backpack")
	bp.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "GoldSkateboard" then
			child:WaitForChild("Handle"):WaitForChild("Mesh").MeshId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=398583688"
			child.Handle.Mesh.TextureId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=398582976"
			child.TextureId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=398583045"
		elseif child.Name == "Merely's Sparkle Time Hoverboard" and not child:FindFirstChild("NewHoverboardTool") then
			local newtool = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects["gear_Merely's Sparkle Time Hoverboard"]["Merely's Sparkle Time Hoverboard"]:clone()
			wait(1/30)
			if child then
				child:Destroy()
				newtool.Parent = bp
			else
				newtool:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end)
	
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeLocalChatMode.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,bool)

	end
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleMutedPlayer.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,targetplrname)
	local mode = plr:FindFirstChild("localchat")
	if mode then
		if mode:FindFirstChild(targetplrname) then
			mode[targetplrname]:Destroy()
		else
			local mark = Instance.new("ObjectValue",mode)
			mark.Name = targetplrname
		end
	end
end

function CheckFreeGamePasses(plr)
	if _G["freepasses"] and _G["admins"] then
		for _,v in pairs(_G["freepasses"]) do
			if v == plr.Name then return true end
		end
		for _,v in pairs(_G["admins"]) do
			if v == plr.Name then return true end
		end
	end
end

function AwardFanclubItems(plr)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		local idstoaward = {}
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil and v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then
				local loopcount = 1
				if v:FindFirstChild("LoopCount") then
					loopcount = v.LoopCount.Value
				end
				for i=1,loopcount do
					table.insert(idstoaward,v.ItemID.Value)
				end
			end
		end
		if #idstoaward > 0 then
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AwardTableOfItems:Fire(plr,idstoaward)
		end
	end
end



function GetDJCompensation(plr, datafolder, datatable)
	local compfolder = datafolder:FindFirstChild("Compensation")
	local success
	if not compfolder:FindFirstChild("djcompensation") then
		if datatable then
			datatable["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] = true
		else
			local marker = Instance.new("Folder")
			marker.Name = "djcompensation"
			marker.Parent = compfolder
			success = pcall(function()
				game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data"):UpdateAsync("maindata", function(tbl)
					if tbl ~= nil then
						tbl["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] = true
					end
					return tbl
				end)
			end)
		end
		if datatable or success then
			return 3000
		end
	end
end

function GetCDCompensation(plr, datafolder, datatable)
	local compfolder = datafolder:FindFirstChild("Compensation")
	local playerobjects = datafolder:FindFirstChild("Objects")
	
	if not compfolder:FindFirstChild("CDcompensation") and not compfolder:FindFirstChild("djcompensation") and playerobjects then
		local refund = 0
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if string.sub(v.Name,1,6) == "music_" then
				if playerobjects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then
					refund = refund + v.ItemCost.Value
				end
			end
		end
		if refund > 0 then
			datatable["Compensation"]["CDcompensation"] = true
		end
		return refund
	end
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetDJCompensation.OnInvoke = function(plr, datafolder)
	return GetDJCompensation(plr, datafolder)
end

function RepeatGet(pd, key, successes, tries)
	local success, reason, returnvalue
	local i = 0
	repeat
		if i ~= 0 then
			wait(3)
		end
		success, reason = pcall(function()
			returnvalue = pd:GetAsync(key)
		end)
		i = i + 1
	until success or i >= (tries or 10)
	
	if successes then
		table.insert(successes,success)
		if debugmode or not success then print(reason) end
	end
	
	return returnvalue, success, reason
end

function RepeatSet(pd, key, value, successes, tries)
	local success, reason, returnvalue
	local i = 0
	repeat
		if i ~= 0 then
			wait(3)
		end
		success, reason = pcall(function()
			returnvalue = pd:SetAsync(key, value)
		end)
		i = i + 1
	until success or i >= (tries or 10)
	
	if successes then
		table.insert(successes,success)
		if debugmode or not success then print(reason) end
	end
	
	return returnvalue, success, reason
end

function RepeatUpdate(pd, key, funct, successes, tries)
	local success, reason, returnvalue
	local i = 0
	repeat
		if i ~= 0 then
			wait(3)
		end
		success, reason = pcall(function()
			returnvalue = pd:UpdateAsync(key, funct)
		end)
		i = i + 1
	until success or i >= (tries or 10)
	
	if successes then
		table.insert(successes,success)
		if debugmode or not success then print(reason) end
	end
	
	return returnvalue, success, reason
end

	--loyalty changes
	
	local function GetLoyaltyData(tbl)
		local changes = false
		local timedata = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData
		local day = timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value
		local endtime = timedata.HolidayStartTime.Value + (day * 86400)
		local timeleft = endtime - timedata.CurrentTime.Value
		local timeleftstring = math.floor(timeleft/3600).."h "..math.floor((timeleft%3600)/60).."m"
		
		local current_lp = tbl[1]
		local lp_reward = 0
		local consec_days = tbl[2]
		local current_spins = tbl[4]
		local firstlogintoday = false
		local lastdayloggedin = tbl[3]
		
		if tbl[3] ~= day then --hasn't logged in today
			firstlogintoday = true
			changes = true
			if day - tbl[3] <= 2 then --logged in yesterday or 2 days before, offer rewards for consec. logins
				tbl[3] = day
				tbl[2] = tbl[2] + 1
				consec_days = tbl[2]
				if tbl[2] >= 5 then
					lp_reward = 1000
				else
					lp_reward = 500 + tbl[2] * 100
				end
				tbl[1] = tbl[1] + lp_reward
				current_lp = tbl[1]
				if consec_days % 5 == 0 then
					tbl[4] = tbl[4] + 2
				end
				current_spins = tbl[4]
			else --didn't log in yesterday, reset counter
				tbl[2] = 1
				consec_days = 1
				lp_reward = 600
				tbl[1] = tbl[1] + lp_reward
				current_lp = tbl[1]
				tbl[3] = day
				current_spins = tbl[4]
			end
		end
		
		--create stats
		local loyaltystats = Instance.new("Folder")
		loyaltystats.Name = "LoyaltyStats"
		
		local lp = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		lp.Name = "LP"
		lp.Value = current_lp
		local oldlp = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		oldlp.Name = "OldLP"
		oldlp.Value = current_lp
		
		local spins = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		spins.Name = "Spins"
		spins.Value = current_spins
		local oldspins = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		oldspins.Name = "OldSpins"
		oldspins.Value = current_spins
		
		local lastday = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		lastday.Name = "LastDayLoggedIn"
		lastday.Value = lastdayloggedin
		
		--store data that is only used to determine what's displayed on GUI
		local rewardstat = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		rewardstat.Name = "LPReward"
		rewardstat.Value = lp_reward
		local daysstat = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		daysstat.Name = "ConsecDays"
		daysstat.Value = consec_days
		local dayendtime = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		dayendtime.Name = "EndTime"
		dayendtime.Value = endtime
		local firstloginval = Instance.new("BoolValue",loyaltystats)
		firstloginval.Name = "FirstLogin"
		firstloginval.Value = firstlogintoday
		local playerloginday = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		playerloginday.Name = "PlayerLoginDay"
		playerloginday.Value = day
		
		loyaltystats.Parent = stats	
		
		return tbl, changes
	end
	
	local loyaltytable, changes = GetLoyaltyData(maindata["loyaltytable"])
	maindata["loyaltytable"] = loyaltytable
	if changes then
		changesmade = true
		changestable["loyaltytable"] = true
	end
	
	
	for _,v in pairs(successes) do
		if v == false then
			safetoload = false
		end
	end
	
	
	--start creating the stats objects
	local options = maindata["options"]
	
	local color = Instance.new("Color3Value")
	color.Value = Color3.new(options["ChatColor"][1],options["ChatColor"][2],options["ChatColor"][3])
	color.Name = "chatcolor"
	color.Parent = player
	
	local volumesetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	volumesetting.Name = "VolumeSetting"
	volumesetting.Value = options["Volume"][1]
	volumesetting.Parent = player
	
	local clubvolumesetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	clubvolumesetting.Name = "ClubVolumeSetting"
	clubvolumesetting.Value = options["Volume"][2]
	clubvolumesetting.Parent = player
	
	local boomboxsetting = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	boomboxsetting.Name = "BoomboxSetting"
	boomboxsetting.Value = options["Volume"][3]
	boomboxsetting.Parent = player
	
	
	local detailsetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	detailsetting.Name = "DetailSetting"
	detailsetting.Value = options["LevelOfDetail"][1]
	detailsetting.Parent = player
	
	local housessetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	housessetting.Name = "HousesSetting"
	housessetting.Value = options["LevelOfDetail"][2]
	housessetting.Parent = player
	
	--add FirstLoginTime to the data object
	
	local flt = Instance.new("IntValue")
	flt.Name = "FirstLoginTime"
	flt.Value = maindata["FirstLoginTime"]
	flt.Parent = stats
	
	
	--handle the DJ recently played and favorites lists
	--two folders for each list: one that holds the actual current list of songs (stringvalues where Name = songID and Value is set by the client to the song's name), and another that stores boolvalues indicating whether to add/remove a song from the list when saving
	
	local songlists = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	songlists.Name = "SongLists"
	
	local recent = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	recent.Name = "RecentlyPlayed"
	local recentchanges = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	recentchanges.Name = "RecentChanges"
	local favorites = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	favorites.Name = "Favorites"
	local favoritechanges = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	favoritechanges.Name = "FavoriteChanges"
	
	for _,v in pairs(maindata["DJRecent"]) do
		local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",recent)
		marker.Name = v 
	end
	for _,v in pairs(maindata["DJFavorites"]) do
		local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",favorites)
		marker.Name = v 
	end
	
	
	local playerobjects

	playerobjects = Instance.new("Folder")
	playerobjects.Name = "Objects"
	playerobjects.Parent = stats
	
	local pendingobjects --objects that will be saved at the autosave interval
	pendingobjects = Instance.new("Folder")
	pendingobjects.Name = "PendingObjects"
	pendingobjects.Parent = stats
	
	local itemdataloader
	pcall(function()
		itemdataloader = require(258548692)
	end)
	if itemdataloader then
		itemdataloader.loadExtraItemData(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects)
	end
	
	
	local oldcarobjects = {} --check these for the old car refund
	
	for i,v in pairs(maindata["objects"]) do --check table of player's objects
		for _,obj in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do --check server objects
			if obj.ItemID.Value == v then --if object's ID == player's item ID
				local marker = Instance.new("StringValue")
				marker.Name = v --marker's name is the ID
				marker.Parent = playerobjects
				if obj:FindFirstChild("OldSchoolCar") then
					table.insert(oldcarobjects,obj)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	
	for _,obj in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if (obj.ItemCost.Value == 0 and not obj:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and not obj:FindFirstChild("NotForSale")) and not playerobjects:FindFirstChild(obj.ItemID.Value) then --give 'em all the free objects
			local marker = Instance.new("StringValue")
			marker.Name = obj.ItemID.Value
			marker.Parent = playerobjects
		end
	end
	
	
	local passes = Instance.new("Folder")
	passes.Name = "GamePasses"
	passes.Parent = stats
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses:GetChildren()) do
		local marker = Instance.new("BoolValue")
		marker.Name = v.Name
		marker.Value = false
		if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,v.Value) or CheckFreeGamePasses(player) == true then
			marker.Value = true
		elseif v.Name == "House" and playerobjects:FindFirstChild("183") then --Custom House product's ItemID
			marker.Value = true
		end
		marker.Parent = passes
	end
	
	
	--track number of purchases for analytics
	local numpurchases = Instance.new("IntValue")
	numpurchases.Name = "NumPurchases"
	local purchcount = 0
	for _,v in pairs(passes:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "House" and v.Value == true then
			purchcount = purchcount + 1
		end
	end
	purchcount = purchcount + #maindata["PurchaseHistory"]
	numpurchases.Value = purchcount
	numpurchases.Parent = stats
	
	
	local outfits = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	outfits.Name = "Outfits"
	local alloutfitdata = maindata["Outfits"]
	
	for i=1,24 do --create all of the outfits in the data
		local outfit = Instance.new("Folder",outfits)
		outfit.Name = i
		
		local outfitdata = alloutfitdata[i]
		
		for x=1,3 do
			local hat = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
			hat.Name = "Hat"..x
			hat.Value = outfitdata["Hat"..x]
		end
		
		local outfitname = Instance.new("StringValue",outfit)
		outfitname.Name = "OutfitName"
		outfitname.Value = outfitdata["OutfitName"]
		local shirt = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		shirt.Name = "Shirt"
		shirt.Value = outfitdata["Shirt"]
		local pants = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		pants.Name = "Pants"
		pants.Value = outfitdata["Pants"]
		local face = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		face.Name = "Face"
		face.Value = outfitdata["Face"]
		local package = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		package.Name = "Package"
		package.Value = outfitdata["Package"]
		local removeshirt = Instance.new("BoolValue",outfit)
		removeshirt.Name = "RemoveShirt"
		removeshirt.Value = outfitdata["RemoveShirt"]
		local rpname = Instance.new("StringValue",outfit)
		rpname.Name = "RPName"
		rpname.Value = outfitdata["RPName"]
		local rpdesc = Instance.new("StringValue",outfit)
		rpdesc.Name = "RPDesc"
		rpdesc.Value = outfitdata["RPDesc"]
	end
	
	
	
	
	--for special flags (whenever we get to that): Special flags structure will be: Folders are tables. For value objects, the name is the i and the value is the v
	
	
	
	--do compensation checks, perform compensation if conditions are met and the flags are missing
	
	--for DJ, need two different compensations! The same old one for having both CR+Unrestricted (but only the 1,000, not the CD bonus) and a separate one just for having the CR DJ pass (CD refund only) (this must ONLY be given if the player doesn't already have the Unrestricted refund)
	
	-- place refund markers inside of a refunds folder, and on the local side, player will wait to display each refund window
	
	local compfolder = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	compfolder.Name = "Compensation"
	
	-- CDs only comp for DJ pass
	if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.ClubRed.Value) and maindata["Compensation"]["CDcompensation"] == nil and maindata["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] == nil and safetoload then
		local incvalue = GetCDCompensation(player, stats, maindata)
		if incvalue and incvalue > 0 then
			maindata["cash"] = maindata["cash"] + incvalue
			changesmade = true
			changestable["Compensation"] = true
			changestable["cash"] = true
			if player then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.DisplayRefundWindow:FireClient(player, "CD", incvalue)
			end
		end
	end
	
	-- +3,000 comp for both passes
	if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.UnrestrictedDJ.Value) and game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.ClubRed.Value) and maindata["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] == nil and safetoload then
		local incvalue = GetDJCompensation(player, stats, maindata)
		if incvalue then
			maindata["cash"] = maindata["cash"] + incvalue
			changesmade = true
			changestable["Compensation"] = true
			changestable["cash"] = true
			if player then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player, "You have received +$"..incvalue.." RHS Cash with your purchase of Unrestricted DJ!",true)
			end
		end
	end
	
	
	
	--old cars comp
	if #oldcarobjects > 0 and safetoload then
		if maindata["Compensation"]["oldcarcomp"] == nil then
			changesmade = true
			changestable["Compensation"] = true
			changestable["cash"] = true
			
			local totalcost = 0
			for _,obj in pairs(oldcarobjects) do
				totalcost = totalcost + obj.ItemCost.Value
			end
			maindata["cash"] = maindata["cash"] + totalcost
			maindata["Compensation"]["oldcarcomp"] = true
			if player then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.DisplayRefundWindow:FireClient(player, "OldCar", totalcost)
			end
		end
	end
	
	for i,_ in pairs(maindata["Compensation"]) do
		local marker = Instance.new("Folder",compfolder)
		marker.Name = i 
	end
	
	
	--finish creating stats objects that are dependent on compensation results
	
	
	local timestat
	timestat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	timestat.Name = "totaltime"
	timestat.Value = totaltime
	timestat.Parent = stats
	
	local cashstat
	cashstat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	cashstat.Name = "cash"
	cashstat.Value = maindata["cash"]
	cashstat.Parent = stats
	
	local oldcashstat
	oldcashstat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	oldcashstat.Name = "oldcash"
	oldcashstat.Value = maindata["cash"]
	oldcashstat.Parent = stats
		
	local furniturestat --holds all furniture counters, removed on quit
	furniturestat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	furniturestat.Name = "FurnitureCost"
	furniturestat.Value = 0
	furniturestat.Parent = stats
	
	local fanclub = Instance.new("BoolValue",stats)
	fanclub.Name = "IsInFanClub"
	fanclub.Value = false
	
	local badgesearned = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	badgesearned.Name = "BadgesEarned"
	
	
	--update the data if: there are no failures, if changes are made, and if not a guest
	local success
	
	--compare origdata and maindata for changes with cash
	if safetoload and changesmade and not isguest then
		maindata, success = RepeatUpdate(pd, "maindata", function(tbl)
			for i,_ in pairs(changestable) do
				if i == "cash" then
					local toadd = maindata["cash"] - origdata["cash"]
					tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + toadd
				elseif i == "objects" then
					--this is only required if there's been a transfer from the old objects format - NEVER overwrite like this just for adding/removing a few objects - if we were doing that, should give a table with a list of objects to add/remove
					tbl["objects"] = maindata["objects"]
				else
					tbl[i] = maindata[i]
				end
			end
			return tbl
		end, successes, 15)
	elseif safetoload and (not changesmade or isguest) then
		success = true
	end
	
	if not success then
		safetoload = false
	end
	
	
	local marker = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	marker.Name = "LoadedSuccessfully"
	
	if safetoload == false then
		marker.Value = false
		if player then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Error: Your data could not be loaded at this time. ROBLOX's servers may be experiencing connection issues. Try rejoining at a later time.",true)
		end
	else
		marker.Value = true
	end
	
	marker.Parent = stats
	
	
	if player ~= nil and player.Parent ~= nil then
		stats.Parent = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData
		if game.PlaceId ~= 9689581 or player:IsInGroup(1127093) then
			fanclub.Value = true
			AwardFanclubItems(player)
		end
	end
	
	BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[1])
	
	--john doe badge Kappa
	if (os.time() > 1489820400 and os.time() < 1489906800) then
		BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,693040536)
	end
	
	
	--LOAD RHS HEADPHONES OWNERSHIP--
	local hastoy = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	hastoy.Name = "HasRHSToy"
	hastoy.Value = false
	pcall(function()
		if game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):PlayerOwnsAsset(player, 501960266) then
			hastoy.Value = true
		end
	end)
	hastoy.Parent = player
	
	
	--IS ROBLOX ADMIN--
	if player:IsInGroup(1200769) then
		local isrobloxadmin = Instance.new("BoolValue")
		isrobloxadmin.Name = "IsRobloxAdmin"
		isrobloxadmin.Value = true
		isrobloxadmin.Parent = player
	end
	
	
	--IS RHS DEV--
	if player:IsInGroup(1127093) and player:GetRankInGroup(1127093) >= 253 then
		local isrhsdev = Instance.new("BoolValue")
		isrhsdev.Name = "IsRHSDev"
		isrhsdev.Value = true
		isrhsdev.Parent = player
	end
	
	
	
	
	
	--BEGIN STATS UPDATING CODE--
	
	
	--COROUTINE TO UPDATE TOTAL TIME + TIMED CASH BONUS--
	
	local timecoroutine
	timecoroutine = coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() 
		local ticktock = 0
		while true do
			wait(.99)
			if player.Parent ~= nil then
				if not (player:FindFirstChild("IdleChecker") == nil or player.IdleChecker.Value == true) then
					ticktock = ticktock + 1
					if ticktock == 60 then
						game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlushData:Fire(player)
					end
					if ticktock == 60 then
						ticktock = 0
						timeleader.Value = timeleader.Value + 1
						local tottime
						pcall(function()
							tottime = pt:IncrementAsync(pid.."_playtime", 1)
							timestat.Value = tottime
						end)
						
						if tottime then
							if tottime >= 300 and not stats.BadgesEarned:FindFirstChild(tostring(badgeids[2])) then
								BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[2])
								local marker = Instance.new("Folder",stats.BadgesEarned)
								marker.Name = badgeids[2]
							end
							
							if tottime >= 1440 and not stats.BadgesEarned:FindFirstChild(tostring(badgeids[3])) then
								BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[3])
								local marker = Instance.new("Folder",stats.BadgesEarned)
								marker.Name = badgeids[3]
							end
							
							if tottime >= 4320 and not stats.BadgesEarned:FindFirstChild(tostring(badgeids[4])) then
								BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[4])
								local marker = Instance.new("Folder",stats.BadgesEarned)
								marker.Name = badgeids[3]
							end
						end
						
						local cashbonus = 2
						if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,cashpass) or (os.time() > starttime and os.time() < endtime) then
							cashbonus = cashbonus + 2
						end
						if game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(player,"VIP") then
							cashbonus = cashbonus + 3
						end
						if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,191991934) and cashstat.Value < 2000000000 then
							cashbonus = 5000
						end
						
						cashstat.Value = cashstat.Value + cashbonus
						
						game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Playtime.Value = game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Playtime.Value + cashbonus
						
					end
				end
			else
				timecoroutine = nil
				coroutine.yield()
			end
		end
	end))
	
	
	--CHARACTERADDED THINGS--
	
	player.CharacterAdded:connect(function(char)
		local ca = Instance.new("StringValue",char)
		ca.Name = "ContextActionsModelName"
		ca.Value = "Player"
	end)
	
	if player.Character and not player.Character:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName") then
		local ca = Instance.new("StringValue",player.Character)
		ca.Name = "ContextActionsModelName"
		ca.Value = "Player"
	end
	
	
	
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.IsDataLoadedSuccessfully.OnInvoke = function(plr)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		if data.LoadedSuccessfully.Value == true then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
end


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeVolume.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,vol,clubvol,boombox)
	plr.VolumeSetting.Value = vol
	plr.ClubVolumeSetting.Value = clubvol
	plr.BoomboxSetting.Value = boombox
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeDetailSetting.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,lod,houses)
	plr.DetailSetting.Value = lod
	plr.HousesSetting.Value = houses
end)


game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlushData.Event:connect(function(plr)
	
	if plr and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		--print("Flushing data for "..plr.Name)
		local isingame = true
		if plr.Parent == nil then
			isingame = false
		end
		local pid = plr.userId
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(pid)
		
		if isingame == false and script.Flushing:FindFirstChild(pid) then
			repeat wait() until script.Flushing:FindFirstChild(pid) == nil
		end
		
		if data and not script.Flushing:FindFirstChild(pid) then
			local marker = Instance.new("Folder",script.Flushing)
			marker.Name = pid
			
			
			--save pending objects first
			--format of a pending object:
			--Name/Value: ItemID. 
			----CurrencyType: "LP" or "cash".
			----ItemCost
			local pendingobjects = data.PendingObjects:GetChildren()
			local objects = data.Objects
			
			--[[
			local coredatatable = {
				["cash"] = 50,
				["options"] = {
					["ChatColor"] = {999,0,0},
					["Volume"] = {7,7,true}, --normal, club, boombox
					["LevelOfDetail"] = {1,1} --LoD, houses enabled (1 = never, 2 = nearby only, 3 = always)
				},
				["objects"] = {},
				["loyaltytable"] = {0,0,0,0}, --LP, consec. days logged in, # of last day logged in, # spins available
				["Outfits"] = {}, --initialize later
				["Compensation"] = {}, --put each comp in here as ["CompName"] = true
				["DJRecent"] = {},
				["DJFavorites"] = {},
				["FirstLoginTime"] = 0,
				["SpecialFlags"] = {"FirstLoginWithNewData"}
			}
			]]
			
			local cash = data.cash.Value
			local oldcash = data.oldcash.Value
			data.oldcash.Value = cash
			local diff = cash - oldcash
			
			local vol = plr.VolumeSetting.Value
			local clubvol = plr.ClubVolumeSetting.Value
			local boombox = plr.BoomboxSetting.Value
			local color = plr.chatcolor
			local lod = plr.DetailSetting.Value
			local hsetting = plr.HousesSetting.Value
			
			local recentchanges = data.SongLists.RecentChanges
			local favoritechanges = data.SongLists.FavoriteChanges
			
			local ls = plr:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
			local tp
			if ls then
				tp = ls:FindFirstChild("Time Played")
			end
			
			local loyaltystats = data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats")
			local lp
			local oldlp
			local spins
			local oldspins
			local lpdiff
			local spinsdiff
			
			if loyaltystats then
				lp = loyaltystats.LP.Value
				oldlp = loyaltystats.OldLP.Value
				spins = loyaltystats.Spins.Value
				oldspins = loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value
				
				lpdiff = lp - oldlp
				spinsdiff = spins - oldspins
				
				loyaltystats.OldLP.Value = lp
				loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value = spins
			end
			
			--update it all in 1 go!
			local success
			local tries = 0
			repeat
				success = pcall(function()
					local update = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data"):UpdateAsync("maindata",function(tbl)
						if tbl == nil then
							tbl = GetCoreDataTable()
						end
						
						--objects
						for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
							table.insert(tbl["objects"],v.Value)
						end
						
						--cash 
						tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + diff
						
						--options
						if ls and tp and tp.Value < 1 then
							if lod > 1 then
								lod = lod - 1
							else
								hsetting = 1
							end
						end
						tbl["options"] = {
							["ChatColor"] = {color.Value.r,color.Value.g,color.Value.b},
							["Volume"] = {vol,clubvol,boombox}, --normal, club, boombox
							["LevelOfDetail"] = {lod,hsetting} --LoD, houses enabled
						}
						
						--loyaltytable
						if loyaltystats then
							tbl["loyaltytable"][1] = tbl["loyaltytable"][1] + lpdiff
							tbl["loyaltytable"][4] = tbl["loyaltytable"][4] + spinsdiff
						end
						
						--outfits
						local outfits = {}
						for i=1,24 do
							local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
							local entry = {
								
								["OutfitName"] = "", --25 char limit
					
								["Hat1"] = 0,
								["Hat2"] = 0,
								["Hat3"] = 0,
								["Shirt"] = 0,
								["Pants"] = 0,
								
								["Face"] = 0,
								["Package"] = 0,
								
								["RPName"] = "",
								["RPDesc"] = "",
								
								["RemoveShirt"] = false
								
							}
							for _,v in pairs(outfit:GetChildren()) do
								entry[v.Name] = v.Value
							end
							table.insert(outfits,entry)
						end
						tbl["Outfits"] = outfits
						
						--compensation
						for _,v in pairs(data.Compensation:GetChildren()) do
							if tbl["Compensation"][v.Name] == nil then
								tbl["Compensation"][v.Name] = true
							end
						end
						
						--DJRecent and DJFavorites changes (true = add, false = remove)
						for _,v in pairs(recentchanges:GetChildren()) do
							for i,x in pairs(tbl["DJRecent"]) do
								if x == v.Name then
									table.remove(tbl["DJRecent"], i)
									break
								end
							end
							if v.Value == true then
								table.insert(tbl["DJRecent"], 1, v.Name)
							end
						end
						for _,v in pairs(favoritechanges:GetChildren()) do
							if v.Value == true then
								table.insert(tbl["DJFavorites"], v.Name)
							else
								for i,x in pairs(tbl["DJFavorites"]) do
									if x == v.Name then
										table.remove(tbl["DJFavorites"], i)
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
						
						
						return tbl
					end)
				end)
				tries = tries + 1
				if not success then wait(2) end
			until success or tries >= 10
			
			
			
			if success then
				for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
					v:Destroy()
				end
				for _,v in pairs(recentchanges:GetChildren()) do
					v:Destroy()
				end
				for _,v in pairs(favoritechanges:GetChildren()) do
					v:Destroy()
				end
			else
				data.oldcash.Value = data.oldcash.Value - diff
				print("Cash change unsuccessful for user "..pid)
				
				if loyaltystats then
					loyaltystats.OldLP.Value = loyaltystats.OldLP.Value - lpdiff
					loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value = loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value - spinsdiff
					print("Loyalty table change unsuccessful for user "..pid)
				end
					
				local arepaidobjects = false
				for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
					if v.ItemCost.Value ~= 0 then
						arepaidobjects = true
						local itemmarker = objects:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
						if itemmarker then
							itemmarker:Destroy()
						end
						local currency = v.CurrencyType.Value == "LP" and data.LoyaltyStats.LP or data.cash
						currency.Value = currency.Value + v.ItemCost.Value
					end
				end
				for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
					if v.ItemCost.Value ~= 0 then
						v:Destroy()
					end
				end
				if arepaidobjects then
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"An error occurred with your recent purchase(s). You have been refunded; please try again later.")

marketplace.ProcessReceipt = function(receiptInfo)
	print("Attempting to process receipt for "..receiptInfo.PlayerId)
	local playerProductKey = receiptInfo.CurrencySpent .. ":" .. receiptInfo.PurchaseId
	
	for i,player in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
		if player.userId == receiptInfo.PlayerId then
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(tostring(player.userId), 10)
			if data then
				
				if data.LoadedSuccessfully.Value == false then
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Cannot process your purchase yet, as ROBLOX's DataStore servers are down. Rejoin the game at a later time to receive your purchased product. Otherwise, you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 72 hours.", true)
					return
				end
				
				print("Granting purchase to "..receiptInfo.PlayerId)
				local pd = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(player.userId.."_data")
				local cashval = 0
				
				for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Value == receiptInfo.ProductId then
						cashval = tonumber(v.Name)
					end
				end
				
				if cashval ~= 0 then
					local processed = false
					local _, success = RepeatUpdate(pd, "maindata", function(tbl)
						if tbl == nil then
							return tbl
						end
						
						for _,v in pairs(tbl["PurchaseHistory"]) do
							if v == playerProductKey then
								processed = true
							end
						end
						
						if not processed then
							table.insert(tbl["PurchaseHistory"], playerProductKey)
							tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + cashval
						end
						
						return tbl
					end)
					
					if processed then
						return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
					end
					
					if success == true then
						if data then
							data.cash.Value = data.cash.Value + cashval
							data.oldcash.Value = data.oldcash.Value + cashval
						end
					else
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Cannot process your purchase yet, as ROBLOX's DataStore servers are down. Rejoin the game at a later time to receive your purchased product. Otherwise, you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 72 hours.", true)
						return
					end
					if player then
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"+$"..cashval.." RHS Cash has been added to your in-game balance!")
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayChachingSound:FireClient(player)
					end
				end
				
				local spcash
				
				if receiptInfo.ProductId == game.ReplicatedStorage.OtherDevProducts.StarterPack.Value then
					if data.Objects:FindFirstChild("318") then
						--already purchased starter pack, notify of refund
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player, "You have somehow purchased a second RHS Starter Pack in error. Your second purchase has not been processed; you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 3 days.", true)
						return
					end
					-- Award starter pack ($7500, Classy Car, furniture set)
					local cashaward = 7500
					local hashouse = true
					local objectstbl = {151,124,124,124,125,125,153,147,259,148,109,146,141,189,189,189, 70,128,127, 108,108,108,108,108,108,108,108, 135, 318} --318 is starter pack item ID
					
					if not data.Objects:FindFirstChild("278") then
						--does not have classy car
						table.insert(objectstbl, 278)
					else
						--already has it
						cashaward = cashaward + 1600
					end
					
					if not data.Objects:FindFirstChild("183") and data.GamePasses.House.Value == false then
						--does not have custom house
						table.insert(objectstbl, 183)
						hashouse = false
					end
					
					local processed = false
					local success = pcall(function()
						local update = pd:UpdateAsync("maindata",function(tbl)
							if tbl == nil then
								return tbl
							end
							
							for _,v in pairs(tbl["PurchaseHistory"]) do
								if v == playerProductKey then
									processed = true
								end
							end
							
							if not processed then
								table.insert(tbl["PurchaseHistory"], playerProductKey)
								tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + cashaward
								for _,itemid in pairs(objectstbl) do
									table.insert(tbl["objects"],itemid)
								end
							end
							return tbl
						end)
					end)
					
					if processed then
						return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
					end
					
					if success then
						if data and data:FindFirstChild("Objects") then
							for _,itemid in pairs(objectstbl) do
								local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",data.Objects)
								marker.Name = itemid
							end
							if data:FindFirstChild("cash") and data:FindFirstChild("oldcash") then
								data.cash.Value = data.cash.Value + cashaward
								data.oldcash.Value = data.oldcash.Value + cashaward
							end
							
							if player and hashouse == false and data:FindFirstChild("GamePasses") then
								--give the custom house
								data.GamePasses.House.Value = true
								game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.LoadHouseForPlayer:Fire(player)
							end
						end
						
						if player then
							if cashaward == 7500 then
								--got car too
								game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"You have received your Starter Pack containing +$"..cashaward.." RHS Cash, the Classy Car, and a set of starting furniture for your Custom House!", true)
							else
								game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"You have received your Starter Pack containing +$"..cashaward.." RHS Cash and a set of starting furniture for your Custom House!", true)
							end
							game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayChachingSound:FireClient(player)
						end
						
						spcash = cashaward
					else
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Cannot process your purchase yet, as ROBLOX's DataStore servers are down. Rejoin the game at a later time to receive your purchased product. Otherwise, you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 72 hours.", true)
						return
					end
				end
				
				if data:FindFirstChild("NumPurchases") then
					local id = (cashval ~= 0 and tostring(cashval) or "StarterPack")
					data.NumPurchases.Value = data.NumPurchases.Value + 1
					GA:SendEvent(
						{
							["category"] = "business",
							["event_id"] = "Cash:Cash"..id,
							["amount"] = math.floor(math.floor(receiptInfo.CurrencySpent * .7) / 4),
							["currency"] = "USD",
							["transaction_num"] = data.NumPurchases.Value,
							["cart_type"] = data.CartType.Value
						}
					)
					
					GA:SendEvent(
						{
							["category"] = "resource",
							["event_id"] = "Source:Cash:RobuxPurchase:"..(spcash and "StarterPack" or "RHSCash"),
							["amount"] = spcash or cashval
						}
					)
				end
				
				print("Dev Product purchase completed for "..receiptInfo.PlayerId)
				return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
				
			end
		end
	end
