-- type: firemachine/smokemachine -- enabled: true/false if turning it on or off, otherwise nil -- color: color if changing color, otherwise nil

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ModifyFireSmokeMachines.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,parttype,enabled,color) 
	if #queue > 0 and queue[1].PlayerName == plr.Name and queue[1].HasUnrestrictedDJ == true then --make sure request is valid
		for _,v in pairs(djstand:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == parttype then
				if enabled ~= nil then
					v.Effect.Enabled = enabled
					if v:FindFirstChild("PointLight") then
						v.PointLight.Enabled = enabled
					end
				end
				if color ~= nil then
					v.Effect.Color = color
					if v:FindFirstChild("PointLight") then
						v.PointLight.Color = color
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

-- Turn off the machines, activated whenever the first song in the queue is removed

function turnOffMachines()
	for i,v in pairs(djstand:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == "FireMachine" then
			v.Effect.Enabled = false
			v.PointLight.Enabled = false
		elseif v.Name == "SmokeMachine" then
			v.Effect.Enabled = false
		end
	end
end

-- Handle requests to Favorite songs (ID passed as parameter; add fav if it doesn't exist, delete fav if it does)

function ToggleFavoriteSong(plr, id)
	local list
	local changes
	local data
	
	data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		data = data.SongLists
		list = data.Favorites
		changes = data.FavoriteChanges
		
		local changeremoved
		
		local change = changes:FindFirstChild(id)
		if change then
			change:Destroy()
			changeremoved = true
		end
		
		--see whether to add or remove favorite
		local entry = list:FindFirstChild(id)
		
		if entry == nil and #list:GetChildren() >= 100 then return end --can't add any more
		
		if not changeremoved then
			local marker = Instance.new("BoolValue",changes)
			marker.Name = id
			marker.Value = entry == nil and true or false
		end
		if entry then
			--remove favorite
			entry:Destroy()
		else
			--add favorite
			local marker2 = Instance.new("StringValue",list)
			marker2.Name = id
		end
	end
end

-- Add song to Recently Played list (if song is already on list, make a clone and delete original)
-- (removing children from the folder and reparenting them places them at the end of the list)

function AddToRecentlyPlayed(plr, id)
	local list
	local changes
	local data
	
	data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		data = data.SongLists
		list = data.RecentlyPlayed
		changes = data.RecentChanges
		
		--add to recently played
		local entry = list:FindFirstChild(id)
		
		local marker = Instance.new("BoolValue",changes)
		marker.Name = id
		marker.Value = true
		
		if entry then
			entry:Destroy()
		end
		
		--add marker to list, parent everything else outside of folder to let new song be at top of list
		for _,v in pairs(list:GetChildren()) do
			v.Parent = script.TempObjectHolder
		end
		local marker2 = Instance.new("StringValue",list)
		marker2.Name = id
		--move objects back into list, but remove any excess songs past 30
		for i,v in pairs(script.TempObjectHolder:GetChildren()) do
			if i < 29 then
				v.Parent = list
			else
				local removal = Instance.new("BoolValue",changes)
				removal.Name = id
				removal.Value = false
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end

-- Listen for requests to toggle favorite songs
game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleFavoriteSong.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr, id)
	if game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(plr,"UnrestrictedDJ") == true and #tostring(id) < 15 then
		ToggleFavoriteSong(plr, id)
	end
end)

-- Add a song (which has already been checked for validity) to the end of the queue. The sound passed is from the validation function which creates the sound.
-- Returns: success (bool), error message if any

function AddSongToQueue(plr, sound, HasUDJ)
	if not (plr and sound) then
		return false, "Error while adding song to queue. Please try again."
	end
	
	local id_digits = sound.SoundId:match("%d+")
	
	if not id_digits then
		return false, "Error while adding song to queue. Please try again."
	end
	
	sound.Volume = 0
	
	local data = Instance.new("Folder")
	data.Name = sound.Name
	
	local id = Instance.new("IntValue", data)
	id.Name = "SongID"
	id.Value = id_digits
	
	local pid = Instance.new("IntValue", data)
	pid.Name = "PlayerID"
	pid.Value = plr.userId
	
	local pname = Instance.new("StringValue", data)
	pname.Name = "PlayerName"
	pname.Value = plr.Name
	
	local queuepos = Instance.new("IntValue", data)
	queuepos.Name = "QueuePosition"
	queuepos.Value = #queue + 1
	
	local queueitem = {
		["PlayerName"] = plr.Name,
		["PlayerID"] = plr.userId,
		["SongID"] = sound.SoundId,
		["SongName"] = sound.Name,
		["HasUnrestrictedDJ"] = HasUDJ,
		["SoundObject"] = sound,
		["QueueObject"] = data,
	}
	
	table.insert(queue, queueitem)
	
	if HasUDJ then
		AddToRecentlyPlayed(plr, tostring(id_digits))
	end
	
	data.Parent = game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems
	sound.Parent = game.ServerStorage.ServerSoundStorage
	
	if queuepos.Value == 1 then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NextSongInQueue:FireAllClients(data)
	end
	
	if sound.TimeLength > 179 and not HasUDJ then
		return true, math.floor(sound.TimeLength)
	end
	
	return true
end

-- Remove a song from queue position n, shifting the queue position of all upcoming songs forward.

function DeleteSongFromQueue(n)
	local queueitem = queue[n]
	if queueitem == nil then return end
	
	local sound = queueitem.SoundObject
	local queueobj = queueitem.QueueObject
	if not (sound and queueobj) then return end
	
	local nextsong
	
	for _,s in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren()) do
		if s.QueuePosition.Value > n then
			s.QueuePosition.Value = s.QueuePosition.Value - 1
			if s.QueuePosition.Value == 1 then
				nextsong = s 
			end
		end
	end
	
	sound:Destroy()
	queueobj:Destroy()
	
	if n == 1 then
		turnOffMachines()
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NextSongInQueue:FireAllClients(nextsong)
	end
	
	table.remove(queue, n)
end

-- Validate whether a song can be played - part of the process of a player submitting a song request to the server.
-- Returns: sound object, success (bool) and an error message, if any.

function ValidateSong(plr, songid)
	local songname
	
	local asset
	pcall(function() asset = game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(songid) end)
	if asset == nil then
		return nil, false, "This ID is invalid and could not be loaded."
	end
	songname = asset.Name
	
	--validate whether the asset fits the appropriate categories to be played
	
	if asset.AssetTypeId ~= 3 then
		return nil, false, "This is not a valid ID for an audio file."
	end

	if asset.IsPublicDomain == false then
		return nil, false, "This audio file is not available in the public domain."
	end	
	--create the sound, wait for the audio file to be fully loaded and check the length
	
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound", game.ServerStorage)
	sound.Name = songname
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..songid
	sound.Looped = true
	sound.Volume = 0
	
	local i = 0
	repeat
		wait(1/30)
		i = i + 1
	until sound.IsLoaded or i >= 240
	
	if not sound.IsLoaded then
		-- sound took too long to load 
		return sound, false, "Server failed to load this song. Please try again later."
	end
	
	if sound.TimeLength < 29 then
		return sound, false, "Songs must be longer than 30 seconds long."
	end
	
	return sound, true
end

-- Checks whether the queue has free space, whether the player is allowed to add more songs to the queue, whether the song ID is already in the queue, and whether the person is banned from adding songs
-- Returns: success (bool) and an error message, if any

function CheckQueueStatus(plr, songid, num_songs_allowed)
	if #queue >= 10 then
		return false, "The song queue is currently full. Please try again later!"
	end
	
	local songcount = 0
	
	for _,id in pairs(banned_userIDs) do
		if plr.userId == id then
			return false, "You have been temporarily restricted due to playing very loud audio."
		end
	end
	
	for _,data in pairs(queue) do
		if data.SongID:match("%d+") == songid then
			return false, "This song is already in the queue. Please pick a different one."
		elseif data.PlayerID == plr.userId then
			songcount = songcount + 1
		end
	end
	
	if songcount >= num_songs_allowed then
		if num_songs_allowed == 2 then
			return false, "Please wait for another one of your songs to finish playing."
		else
			return false, "The Unrestricted DJ pass is required to queue 2 songs at once."
		end
	end
	
	return true
end

-- When the client says it has purchased the UDJ pass, wait and see if that is actually true.

function ValidateUDJPurchase(plr)
	local HasUDJ
	local i = 0
	repeat
		wait(1/3)
		i = i + 1
		HasUDJ = check:Invoke(plr,"UnrestrictedDJ")
	until HasUDJ or i >= 15
	
	return HasUDJ
end

-- Handles the network request from the player requesting to add a song to the queue. Main function responsible for adding songs.
-- Returns: success (bool) and an error message, if any, to the client.

function RequestSong(plr, songid)
	local HasCR = check:Invoke(plr,"ClubRed")
	local HasUDJ = check:Invoke(plr,"UnrestrictedDJ")
	
	if HasCR or HasUDJ then
		--player has pass
		
		--check if this song ID is allowed to be placed in the queue by this user 
		local success, reason, doprompt = CheckQueueStatus(plr, songid, (HasUDJ and 2 or 1))
		
		if not success then
			return success, reason
		end
		
		--validate the song 
		local sound, success, reason = ValidateSong(plr, songid)
		
		if not (sound and success) then
			--failed to validate song; destroy the sound and return the reason to the player
			if sound then
				sound:Destroy()
			end
			return success, reason
		end
		
		
		-- Call the queue checking function again just in case, and add the song to the queue.
		local success, reason, doprompt = CheckQueueStatus(plr, songid, (HasUDJ and 2 or 1))
		
		if not success then
			return success, reason
		end
		
		-- Everything's good. Add the song to the queue.
		
		return AddSongToQueue(plr, sound, HasUDJ)
		
	else
		-- exploiter
		return false, "Nope."
	end
end

-- Remotefunction handling song ID requests to add to the queue.

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestDJSong.OnServerInvoke = function(plr, songid)
	return RequestSong(plr, songid)
end


-- List of requests from players saying that the current song is too loud.
local tooloudrequests = {}

-- RemoteEvent that listens for clients saying that the current song is too loud

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagSong.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr)
	-- check if requesting player is already on list
	for _,v in pairs(tooloudrequests) do
		if v == plr.userId then
			return
		end
	end
	
	if #queue > 0 then
		table.insert(tooloudrequests, plr.userId)
	end
end)

-- Function that allows the main loop to check whether the song is flagged as too loud (575+ loudness)

function SongIsTooLoud(currentsong)
	if #tooloudrequests >= 6 then
		return true
	end
	
	for _,v in pairs(tooloudrequests) do
		if v == currentsong.PlayerID then
			return true
		end
	end
	
	return false
end

-- Add a user to the banned_userIDs table if they have played a very loud song 

function AddUserToBannedList(pid)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		table.insert(banned_userIDs, pid)
		wait(185)
		table.remove(banned_userIDs, 1)
	end))
end


-- Run the loop which manages the playing, timing, and removal of songs

local currentsong --points to the table entry of the current song that's playing
local targettime --the amount of time the song is allowed to play
local elapsedtime = 0

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetTimeOfSong.OnServerInvoke = function(plr)
	if currentsong and currentsong.SoundObject then
		return currentsong.SoundObject.TimePosition
	end
end

-- If player purchases UDJ, set their songs to UDJ true.

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.SetSongsInQueueToUDJ.Event:connect(function(plr)
	for _,queueitem in pairs(queue) do
		if queueitem["PlayerName"] == plr.Name then
			queueitem["HasUDJ"] = true
		end
	end
	if currentsong and currentsong["PlayerName"] == plr.Name and currentsong.SoundObject then
		--set currently playing song to appropriate length
		targettime = currentsong.SoundObject.TimeLength - 3
		if targettime > 418 then
			targettime = 417
		end
	end
end)

--debuggin purposes
game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.PrintBannedDJs.Event:connect(function()
	for _,v in pairs(banned_userIDs) do
		print(game.Players:GetPlayerByUserId(v))
	end
end)

while true do
	local time_passed = wait()
	
	-- If there is a new song in position 1 of the queue, set that to the current song
	if currentsong ~= queue[1] then
		elapsedtime = 0
		oldtime = 0
		if #queue > 0 then
			currentsong = queue[1]
			wait(2.7)
			tooloudrequests = {}
		end
	end
	
	-- Keep checking the current song's conditions
	if currentsong then
		elapsedtime = elapsedtime + time_passed
		
		local sound = currentsong.SoundObject
		if sound then
			if not sound.IsPlaying then
				-- Initialize the song to be played
				sound:Play()
				
				if sound.TimeLength > 120 and not currentsong.HasUnrestrictedDJ then
					targettime = 117
				else
					targettime = sound.TimeLength - 3
					if targettime > 418 then
						targettime = 417
					end
				end
			end
			
			if elapsedtime >= targettime then
				-- End the current song
				DeleteSongFromQueue(1)
				currentsong = nil
			end
			
			if currentsong and SongIsTooLoud(currentsong) then
				-- Punish user if their song is too loud
				local pid = currentsong.PlayerID
				local player = game.Players:FindFirstChild(currentsong.PlayerName)
				if player then
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player, "Warning: Do not play extremely loud audio! Please preview your songs before playing them to make sure they are not loud or distorted.", true)
				end
				AddUserToBannedList(currentsong.PlayerID)
				DeleteSongFromQueue(1)
				currentsong = nil
				for i,v in pairs(queue) do
					if v.PlayerID == pid then
						DeleteSongFromQueue(i)
					end
				end
			end
			
		end
		
	end
end

--routinely push the stats to the GA module

--the push functions (which can also be utilized during OnClose)

function PushShopPurchases()
	for _,v in pairs(game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.ShopPurchases:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Value > 0 then
			local shopname = string.gsub(v.Name,"%W","")
			GA:SendEvent(
				{
					["category"] = "resource",
					["event_id"] = "Source:FoodCount:"..shopname..":Count",
					["amount"] = v.Value
				}
			)
			v.Value = 0
		end
	end
end

function PushTimeSpent()
	for _,v in pairs(game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.TimeSpent:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Value > 2 then
			local shopname = string.gsub(v.Name,"%W","")
			local timeval = math.floor(v.Value / 3)
			GA:SendEvent(
				{
					["category"] = "resource",
					["event_id"] = "Source:TimeSpent:"..shopname..":Mins",
					["amount"] = timeval
				}
			)
			v.Value = 0
		end
	end
end

function PushCashEarned()
	for _,v in pairs(game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Value > 0 then
			local shopname = string.gsub(v.Name,"%W","")
			GA:SendEvent(
				{
					["category"] = "resource",
					["event_id"] = "Source:Cash:"..shopname..":Earnings",
					["amount"] = v.Value
				}
			)
			v.Value = 0
		end
	end
end


--shop purchases

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while wait(60 * 13) do
		PushShopPurchases()
	end
end))

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while wait(60 * 21) do
		PushTimeSpent()
	end
end))

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while wait(60 * 17) do
		PushCashEarned()
	end
end))


game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushShopPurchases.OnInvoke = function()
	PushShopPurchases()
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushCashEarned.OnInvoke = function()
	PushCashEarned()
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushTimeSpent.OnInvoke = function()
	PushTimeSpent()
end

local restrictions = script.Parent.Parent.Restrictions
local editing = script.Parent.Editing

local notificationsenabled = true
local msgenabled = true

function NotifyOwner(invader)
	if notificationsenabled then
		notificationsenabled = false
		local ownername = string.sub(script.Parent.Parent.Name:match("_.+"),2)
		if ownername then
			local owner = game.Players:FindFirstChild(ownername)
			if owner then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(owner, invader.Name.." tried to enter your house, but you are still in Edit Mode. (Save & Exit to allow visitors to enter)")
			end
		end
		wait(75)
		notificationsenabled = true
	end
end

function SendMessage(plr)
	if msgenabled then
		msgenabled = false
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"You are restricted from entering the house at this time. The owner may be in Edit Mode.")
		wait(2)
		msgenabled = true
	end
end

function onHit(hit)
	if hit then
		if hit.Parent.Name:match("vehicle_") then
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.DestroyCar:Fire(hit.Parent,"It's not very polite to smash your car into other people's houses!")
		else
			local plr = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(hit.Parent)
			if plr ~= nil then
				if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
					if (editing.Value == true and plr.Name ~= string.sub(script.Parent.Parent.Name,7)) or (restrictions.Value == true and not restrictions:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) and plr.Name ~= string.sub(script.Parent.Parent.Name,7)) then
						plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = script.Parent.Parent.Door.CFrame + (script.Parent.Parent.Door.CFrame.lookVector * 10)
						
						coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(SendMessage),plr)

						if editing.Value == true then
							NotifyOwner(plr)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

for _,v in pairs(script.Parent.Parent:GetChildren()) do
	if v.Name:match("Floor%d+") or v.Name == "ExteriorWall" or v.Name == "Ceiling" or v.Name == "Door" then
		v.Touched:connect(function(hit)
			onHit(hit)
		end)
	end

if Configuration then-- if we found an object named Configuration in the car then do this stuff:
	local ReverseSpeedValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Reverse Speed")-- do we have a reverse speed?
	if ReverseSpeedValue ~= nil then
		if type(ReverseSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then
			reverseSpeed = ReverseSpeedValue.Value
		end
		ReverseSpeedValue.Changed:connect(function()-- level 1 scripting: go
			if ReverseSpeedValue.Parent ~= nil then-- this script works, but there are some things
				if type(ReverseSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then-- to make it look a little better
					reverseSpeed = ReverseSpeedValue.Value
					UpdateVehicle()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	local ForwardsSpeedValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Forwards Speed")
	if ForwardsSpeedValue then
		if type(ForwardsSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then
			forwardsSpeed = ForwardsSpeedValue.Value
		end
		ForwardsSpeedValue.Changed:connect(function()-- level 2 scripting: go
			if ForwardsSpeedValue.Parent then -- asking if something 'is' is the same as 
				if ForwardsSpeedValue.Value then-- asking if it is "~= nil"
					forwardsSpeed = ForwardsSpeedValue.Value
					UpdateVehicle()
				end		
			end
		end)
	end
	
	local AllowFlippingValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Allow Flipping")
	if AllowFlippingValue then
		if type(AllowFlippingValue.Value)=="boolean" then
			allowFlipping = AllowFlippingValue.Value
		end											-- level 3 scripting: go
		AllowFlippingValue.Changed:connect(function()-- the script executes a chain of 
			if AllowFlippingValue.Parent and type(AllowFlippingValue.Value) == "boolean" then-- "and" statements in the order they
				allowFlipping = AllowFlippingValue.Value-- come in so if the first one isn't "true" then
														-- it will skip the rest
			end
		end)
	end
	local TorqueValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Torque")
	if TorqueValue then
		if type(TorqueValue.Value)=="number" and RemoteControlled then
			RemoteControlled.Torque = TorqueValue.Value
		end
		TorqueValue.Changed:connect(function()
			if TorqueValue.Parent and type(TorqueValue.Value) == "number" and RemoteControlled then
				RemoteControlled.Torque = TorqueValue.Value
			end
		end)
	end
end

script.CarSteering.Seat.Value = seat

if not RemoteControlled:IsA("VehicleSeat") then-- how dare you try to disturb this ancient harmony
	RemoteControlled = nil-- no food 4 u!
end

seat.ChildAdded:connect(function(it)-- something was added to our seat!
	if it:IsA("Weld") and it.Name == "SeatWeld" then-- is it the kind of thing players use to sit with?
		Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(it.Part1.Parent)-- is there a player attached to it?
		if Player then-- hooray! a player WAS attached to the seat weld. It's time to start the car!
			if car.DriverLocked.Value and Player.Name ~= car.Owner.Value then
				wait(1/30)
				if it and it.Part1 and it.Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					it.Part1.Parent.Humanoid.Jump = true
					return
				end
			end
			script.CarSteering:Clone().Parent = Player.Backpack-- for better driving
			RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = 0-- not yet little child
			if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Starting") and engineBlock.Starting:IsA("Sound") then
				engineBlock.Starting:Play()
			end
			if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Stopping") and engineBlock.Stopping:IsA("Sound") then
				engineBlock.Stopping:Stop()
			end
			wait(StartTime)-- just in case you use different starting sound effects
			if it.Parent ~= seat then-- DID OUR LOVELY PLAYER LEAVE WHILE WE WEREN'T LOOKING???????
				byebye()--maybe they did, so it's time to shut down the car
			else-- they didn't leave!
				seat:SetNetworkOwner(Player)
				lightsOn = true-- turn on the lights
				carRunning = true-- turn on the car
				updatelights()-- make sure we turn on the lights
				UpdateVehicle()-- maybe they're in a hurry, so we better check if they have their foot on the gas
				if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Running") and engineBlock.Running:IsA("Sound") then
					engineBlock.Running:Play()
					engineBlock.Running.Pitch = CalculatePitch()
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

seat.ChildRemoved:connect(function(it)--did an object get removed?
	if it:isA("Weld") and it.Name == "SeatWeld" then-- if it's a weld and named like a seat weld
		byebye()-- time to shut down the car
		seat:SetNetworkOwnershipAuto()
	end
end)

function byebye()
	if engineBlock and carRunning then-- remember scripting level 3? that applies to below:
		if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Stopping") and engineBlock.Stopping:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Stopping:Play()-- where if it didn't do it like that, the above would error!
			wait(0.1)					-- because engineBlock.Stopping would not exist, so :IsA() is
		end								-- "not a valid member"
		if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Running") and engineBlock.Running:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Running:Stop()-- WE MUST NOT STOP
		end
		if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Starting") and engineBlock.Starting:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Starting:Stop()-- THIS SCRIPT IS BREAK-PROOF >:U
		end
	end
	RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = 0--make sure that the car doesn't drive off without us
	carRunning = false
	lightsOn = false
	updatelights()--turn the lights off
	UpdateVehicle()--make sure the car stops now that it's off
end

RearLight = {}--all the brake lights. These contain one SpotLight that gets manipulated
RearBulb = {}--the brake lights that use a Surface GUI to light up
FrontLight = {}--same as the RearLight but for the front
FrontBulb = {}--same as the RearBulb but for the front
ReverseLight = {}--These light up when you reverse
ReverseBulb = {}--these also light up when you reverse: SurfaceGui style

for _, i in pairs (car:GetChildren()) do--populate the tables for ease of modularity.
	if i.Name == "RearBulb" then-- you can have any number of these items you want
		table.insert(RearBulb,i)-- and you don't have to change the script one bit!
	elseif i.Name == "RearLight" then
		table.insert(RearLight,i.SpotLight)
	elseif i.Name == "FrontLight" then
		table.insert(FrontLight,i.SpotLight)
	elseif i.Name == "ReverseLight" then
		table.insert(ReverseLight,i.SpotLight)
	elseif i.Name == "FrontBulb" then
		table.insert(FrontBulb,i)
	elseif i.Name == "ReverseBulb" then
		table.insert(ReverseBulb,i)
	elseif i:IsA("VehicleSeat") and i  ~= seat then
		i.ChildAdded:connect(function(it)-- something was added to our seat!
			if it:IsA("Weld") and it.Name == "SeatWeld" then-- is it the kind of thing players use to sit with?
				local plr = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(it.Part1.Parent)
				if plr then
					if car.SeatsLocked.Value and plr.Name ~= car.Owner.Value then
						wait(2/30)
						if it and it.Part1 and it.Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
							it.Part1.Parent.Humanoid.Jump = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

function updatelights()-- this turns the lights on and off based on the lightsOn value
	for _,i in pairs (RearLight) do
		i.Enabled = lightsOn
	end
	for _,i in pairs (FrontLight) do
		i.Enabled = lightsOn
	end
	for _,i in pairs (FrontBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiStandard(i,lightsOn)
	end
	for _,g in pairs (RearBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiBrightness(g,lightsOn,seat.Throttle == 0)
	end
end

flippingDebounce = false-- a debounce is a simple way of preventing a function from
						-- being called multiple times while it's still running
function Flip()
	if not flippingDebounce then-- here's how it works: the function checks if the debounce is off
		flippingDebounce = true-- if it's on, then the function stops, but if it's off, set it to true
		local bodypos = Instance.new("BodyPosition",seat)-- so we get uninterupted function fun
		bodypos.maxForce = Vector3.new(100000,10000000,100000)
		bodypos.position = seat.Position + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
		local bodygyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro",seat)
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(bodypos, 3)-- this makes sure that the flippy bit
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(bodygyro, 3)-- deletes itself automatically
		wait(3)
		flippingDebounce = false-- when we are done with our fun, allow the function to work again
	end
end

function SurfaceGuiStandard(part,on)-- this makes it so the lights turn on or off
	for _,i in pairs (part:GetChildren()) do
		if i:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
			i.Enabled = on
		end
	end
end

function SurfaceGuiBrightness(part,on,bright)-- this is so the brake lights dim when we drive
	for _,i in pairs (part:GetChildren()) do
		if i:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
			i.Enabled = on
			if i:FindFirstChild("Frame") and i.Frame:IsA("Frame") then
				i.Frame.Transparency = bright and 0 or 0.5-- scripting level 4: go
			end-- for simple statements, you can have a boolean value decide between 2 values
			-- which is the same as writing	
			-- "if bright then
			--		i.Frame.Transparency = 0
			--	else
			--		i.Frame.Transparency = 0.5
			-- end"
			--but instead of "then" and "else", you use "and" and "or"
		end
	end
end

hornDebounce = false
function Honk()-- if you are reading this then congratulations! you win a prize!
	if hornDebounce then return end-- unfortunately, I could not think of a way
	if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Horn") and engineBlock.Horn:IsA("Sound") then
		hornDebounce = true-- to allow people to be able to honk the horn for both PC and mobile
		engineBlock.horn:Play()	-- in an unobtrusive way. Sorry! :(
		print("beep beep") -- UristMcSparks, the guy in charge of the toolbox, doesn't want print statements
		wait(0.5)-- but since this is a secret, lets keep it between you and me, OK?
		engineBlock.horn:Stop()
		hornDebounce = false
	end
end

function UpdateVehicle()-- this is for the brake lights and speed and stuff like that
	if seat.Torque ~= 0 and type(seat.Torque) == "number" and RemoteControlled then
		RemoteControlled.Torque = math.abs(seat.Torque)
		seat.Torque = 0
	else
		seat.Torque = 0
	end
	if seat.Throttle == 1 and carRunning then-- if we're going forwards
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = forwardsSpeed-- give it forwards speed
	elseif seat.Throttle == -1 and carRunning then-- if we're going backwards
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = reverseSpeed-- give it backwards speed
	else
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = 0 -- otherwise, give it 0 speed
	end
	if workspace.FilteringEnabled and RemoteControlled and RemoteControlled.Parent then--the idea from scripting level 2 applies to false as well
		RemoteControlled.Throttle = seat.Throttle-- that is, it also works for "~= false"
	end-- the reason this line exists is to prevent the server from doing something the client
	-- is already doing.
	for _,h in pairs (ReverseLight) do-- all these loops below are just updating the lights
		h.Enabled = lightsOn and seat.Throttle == -1
	end
	for _,i in pairs (ReverseBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiStandard(i,lightsOn and seat.Throttle == -1)
	end
	for _,j in pairs (RearLight) do
		j.Brightness = 4-math.abs(seat.Throttle)*3
	end
	for _,k in pairs (RearBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiBrightness(k,lightsOn,seat.Throttle == 0)
	end
end

seat.Changed:connect(UpdateVehicle)-- this is so that when someone drives the car
								   -- the brake and reverse lights change
								   -- and the speed of the car changes

while true do
	for i = 1, 60 do--60/30 == 2, 2 seconds between deciding if it's upside down
		wait()-- we want the steering and sfx to update as many times a second as we can
		if workspace.FilteringEnabled then
			if car:FindFirstChild("LeftMotor") then
				car.LeftMotor.DesiredAngle = seat.Steer*math.rad(40-RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/4)
			end
			if car:FindFirstChild("RightMotor") then
				car.RightMotor.DesiredAngle = seat.Steer*math.rad(40-RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/4)
			end
		end
		-- below: make sure there's a sound to play
		if RemoteControlled and carRunning and engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Running") and engineBlock.Running:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Running.Pitch = CalculatePitch()-- and make sure that the car is on before trying
												-- to make sure the engine sounds good
		end
	end
	--         V this allows people to say if they want the car to flip over or not
	if allowFlipping and (seat.CFrame*CFrame.Angles(math.pi/2,0,0)).lookVector.Y < 0.2 then--am I upside down?
		Flip()--flip the car right side up
		
		-- as a fun experiment, see what happens if you replace "Flip()" with "Spawn(Flip)"
		-- and see what the difference is when you drive the car off the baseplate
		-- if you did it right, you'll notice that the engine sound changes differently!
	end
end

Players = game:GetService("Players")

seat = script.Parent-- the driving seat
car = seat.Parent

Player = nil

forwardsSpeed = 60--if you removed the Configuration folder, it defaults to this speed forwards
reverseSpeed = 20--if you removed the Configuration folder, it defaults to this speed backwards
allowFlipping = true--if you removed the Configuration folder, it defaults to automatically flipping
StartTime = 2.7--how long (in seconds) the car should wait before starting up. This is dependant on the starting sfx

engineBlock = car:FindFirstChild("EngineBlock")--maybe someone doesn't like sound?

lightsOn = false-- whether or not the lights turn on
carRunning = false--whether or not the car is running

RemoteControlled = car:FindFirstChild("ControlByRemote")--this is so the car turns properly

function CalculatePitch()--this returns the pitch that the engine should be at when its travelling
	return 1+RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/100
end

Configuration = car:FindFirstChild("Configuration")-- so you can change the stats of the car
if Configuration then-- if we found an object named Configuration in the car then do this stuff:
	local ReverseSpeedValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Reverse Speed")-- do we have a reverse speed?
	if ReverseSpeedValue ~= nil then
		if type(ReverseSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then
			reverseSpeed = ReverseSpeedValue.Value
		end
		ReverseSpeedValue.Changed:connect(function()-- level 1 scripting: go
			if ReverseSpeedValue.Parent ~= nil then-- this script works, but there are some things
				if type(ReverseSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then-- to make it look a little better
					reverseSpeed = ReverseSpeedValue.Value
					UpdateVehicle()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	local ForwardsSpeedValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Forwards Speed")
	if ForwardsSpeedValue then
		if type(ForwardsSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then
			forwardsSpeed = ForwardsSpeedValue.Value
		end
		ForwardsSpeedValue.Changed:connect(function()-- level 2 scripting: go
			if ForwardsSpeedValue.Parent then -- asking if something 'is' is the same as 
				if ForwardsSpeedValue.Value then-- asking if it is "~= nil"
					forwardsSpeed = ForwardsSpeedValue.Value
					UpdateVehicle()
				end		
			end
		end)
	end
	
	local AllowFlippingValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Allow Flipping")
	if AllowFlippingValue then
		if type(AllowFlippingValue.Value)=="boolean" then
			allowFlipping = AllowFlippingValue.Value
		end											-- level 3 scripting: go
		AllowFlippingValue.Changed:connect(function()-- the script executes a chain of 
			if AllowFlippingValue.Parent and type(AllowFlippingValue.Value) == "boolean" then-- "and" statements in the order they
				allowFlipping = AllowFlippingValue.Value-- come in so if the first one isn't "true" then
														-- it will skip the rest
			end
		end)
	end
	local TorqueValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Torque")
	if TorqueValue then
		if type(TorqueValue.Value)=="number" and RemoteControlled then
			RemoteControlled.Torque = TorqueValue.Value
		end
		TorqueValue.Changed:connect(function()
			if TorqueValue.Parent and type(TorqueValue.Value) == "number" and RemoteControlled then
				RemoteControlled.Torque = TorqueValue.Value
			end
		end)
	end
end

flippingDebounce = false-- a debounce is a simple way of preventing a function from
						-- being called multiple times while it's still running
function Flip()
	if not flippingDebounce then-- here's how it works: the function checks if the debounce is off
		flippingDebounce = true-- if it's on, then the function stops, but if it's off, set it to true
		local bodypos = Instance.new("BodyPosition",seat)-- so we get uninterupted function fun
		bodypos.maxForce = Vector3.new(100000,10000000,100000)
		bodypos.position = seat.Position + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
		local bodygyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro",seat)
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(bodypos, 3)-- this makes sure that the flippy bit
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(bodygyro, 3)-- deletes itself automatically
		wait(3)
		flippingDebounce = false-- when we are done with our fun, allow the function to work again
	end
end

end

			i.Enabled = on
			if i:FindFirstChild("Frame") and i.Frame:IsA("Frame") then
				i.Frame.Transparency = bright and 0 or 0.5-- scripting level 4: go
			end-- for simple statements, you can have a boolean value decide between 2 values
			-- which is the same as writing	
			-- "if bright then
			--		i.Frame.Transparency = 0
			--	else
			--		i.Frame.Transparency = 0.5
			-- end"
			--but instead of "then" and "else", you use "and" and "or"
		end
	end
end

function UpdateVehicle()-- this is for the brake lights and speed and stuff like that
	if seat.Torque ~= 0 and type(seat.Torque) == "number" and RemoteControlled then
		RemoteControlled.Torque = math.abs(seat.Torque)
		seat.Torque = 0
	else
		seat.Torque = 0
	end
	if seat.Throttle == 1 and carRunning then-- if we're going forwards
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = forwardsSpeed-- give it forwards speed
	elseif seat.Throttle == -1 and carRunning then-- if we're going backwards
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = reverseSpeed-- give it backwards speed
	else
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = 0 -- otherwise, give it 0 speed
	end
	if workspace.FilteringEnabled and RemoteControlled and RemoteControlled.Parent then--the idea from scripting level 2 applies to false as well
		RemoteControlled.Throttle = seat.Throttle-- that is, it also works for "~= false"
	end-- the reason this line exists is to prevent the server from doing something the client
	-- is already doing.
	for _,h in pairs (ReverseLight) do-- all these loops below are just updating the lights
		h.Enabled = lightsOn and seat.Throttle == -1
	end
	for _,i in pairs (ReverseBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiStandard(i,lightsOn and seat.Throttle == -1)
	end
	for _,j in pairs (RearLight) do
		j.Brightness = 4-math.abs(seat.Throttle)*3
	end
	for _,k in pairs (RearBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiBrightness(k,lightsOn,seat.Throttle == 0)
	end
end

seat.Changed:connect(UpdateVehicle)-- this is so that when someone drives the car
								   -- the brake and reverse lights change
								   -- and the speed of the car changes

while true do
	for i = 1, 60 do--60/30 == 2, 2 seconds between deciding if it's upside down
		wait()-- we want the steering and sfx to update as many times a second as we can
		if workspace.FilteringEnabled then
			if car:FindFirstChild("LeftMotor") then
				car.LeftMotor.DesiredAngle = seat.Steer*math.rad(40-RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/4)
			end
			if car:FindFirstChild("RightMotor") then
				car.RightMotor.DesiredAngle = seat.Steer*math.rad(40-RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/4)
			end
		end
		-- below: make sure there's a sound to play
		if RemoteControlled and carRunning and engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Running") and engineBlock.Running:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Running.Pitch = CalculatePitch()-- and make sure that the car is on before trying
												-- to make sure the engine sounds good
		end
	end
	--         V this allows people to say if they want the car to flip over or not
	if allowFlipping and (seat.CFrame*CFrame.Angles(math.pi/2,0,0)).lookVector.Y < 0.2 then--am I upside down?
		Flip()--flip the car right side up
		
		-- as a fun experiment, see what happens if you replace "Flip()" with "Spawn(Flip)"
		-- and see what the difference is when you drive the car off the baseplate
		-- if you did it right, you'll notice that the engine sound changes differently!
	end
end

--MADE BY NWSPACEK
wait(0.1)
Players = game:GetService("Players")

seat = script.Parent-- the driving seat
car = seat.Parent

Player = nil

forwardsSpeed = 60--if you removed the Configuration folder, it defaults to this speed forwards
reverseSpeed = 20--if you removed the Configuration folder, it defaults to this speed backwards
allowFlipping = true--if you removed the Configuration folder, it defaults to automatically flipping
StartTime = 1.2--how long (in seconds) the car should wait before starting up. This is dependant on the starting sfx

engineBlock = car:FindFirstChild("EngineBlock")--maybe someone doesn't like sound?

lightsOn = false-- whether or not the lights turn on
carRunning = false--whether or not the car is running

RemoteControlled = car:FindFirstChild("ControlByRemote")--this is so the car turns properly

function CalculatePitch()--this returns the pitch that the engine should be at when its travelling
	return 1+RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/100
end

Configuration = car:FindFirstChild("Configuration")-- so you can change the stats of the car
if Configuration then-- if we found an object named Configuration in the car then do this stuff:
	local ReverseSpeedValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Reverse Speed")-- do we have a reverse speed?
	if ReverseSpeedValue ~= nil then
		if type(ReverseSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then
			reverseSpeed = ReverseSpeedValue.Value
		end
		ReverseSpeedValue.Changed:connect(function()-- level 1 scripting: go
			if ReverseSpeedValue.Parent ~= nil then-- this script works, but there are some things
				if type(ReverseSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then-- to make it look a little better
					reverseSpeed = ReverseSpeedValue.Value
					UpdateVehicle()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	local ForwardsSpeedValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Forwards Speed")
	if ForwardsSpeedValue then
		if type(ForwardsSpeedValue.Value) == "number" then
			forwardsSpeed = ForwardsSpeedValue.Value
		end
		ForwardsSpeedValue.Changed:connect(function()-- level 2 scripting: go
			if ForwardsSpeedValue.Parent then -- asking if something 'is' is the same as 
				if ForwardsSpeedValue.Value then-- asking if it is "~= nil"
					forwardsSpeed = ForwardsSpeedValue.Value
					UpdateVehicle()
				end		
			end
		end)
	end
	
	local AllowFlippingValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Allow Flipping")
	if AllowFlippingValue then
		if type(AllowFlippingValue.Value)=="boolean" then
			allowFlipping = AllowFlippingValue.Value
		end											-- level 3 scripting: go
		AllowFlippingValue.Changed:connect(function()-- the script executes a chain of 
			if AllowFlippingValue.Parent and type(AllowFlippingValue.Value) == "boolean" then-- "and" statements in the order they
				allowFlipping = AllowFlippingValue.Value-- come in so if the first one isn't "true" then
														-- it will skip the rest
			end
		end)
	end
	local TorqueValue = Configuration:FindFirstChild("Torque")
	if TorqueValue then
		if type(TorqueValue.Value)=="number" and RemoteControlled then
			RemoteControlled.Torque = TorqueValue.Value
		end
		TorqueValue.Changed:connect(function()
			if TorqueValue.Parent and type(TorqueValue.Value) == "number" and RemoteControlled then
				RemoteControlled.Torque = TorqueValue.Value
			end
		end)
	end
end

script.CarSteering.Seat.Value = seat

if not RemoteControlled:IsA("VehicleSeat") then-- how dare you try to disturb this ancient harmony
	RemoteControlled = nil-- no food 4 u!

seat.ChildAdded:connect(function(it)-- something was added to our seat!
	if it:IsA("Weld") and it.Name == "SeatWeld" then-- is it the kind of thing players use to sit with?
		Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(it.Part1.Parent)-- is there a player attached to it?
		if Player then-- hooray! a player WAS attached to the seat weld. It's time to start the car!
			if car.DriverLocked.Value and Player.Name ~= car.Owner.Value then
				wait(1/30)
				if it and it.Part1 and it.Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					it.Part1.Parent.Humanoid.Jump = true
					return
				end
			end
			script.CarSteering:Clone().Parent = Player.Backpack-- for better driving
			RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = 0-- not yet little child
			if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Starting") and engineBlock.Starting:IsA("Sound") then
				engineBlock.Starting:Play()
			end
			if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Stopping") and engineBlock.Stopping:IsA("Sound") then
				engineBlock.Stopping:Stop()
			end
			wait(StartTime)-- just in case you use different starting sound effects
			if it.Parent ~= seat then-- DID OUR LOVELY PLAYER LEAVE WHILE WE WEREN'T LOOKING???????
				byebye()--maybe they did, so it's time to shut down the car
			else-- they didn't leave!
				seat:SetNetworkOwner(Player)
				lightsOn = true-- turn on the lights
				carRunning = true-- turn on the car
				updatelights()-- make sure we turn on the lights
				UpdateVehicle()-- maybe they're in a hurry, so we better check if they have their foot on the gas
				if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Running") and engineBlock.Running:IsA("Sound") then
					engineBlock.Running:Play()
					engineBlock.Running.Pitch = CalculatePitch()
				end
				if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("WindSound") then
					engineBlock.WindSound:Play()
					engineBlock.WindSound.Pitch = CalculatePitch()
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

seat.ChildRemoved:connect(function(it)--did an object get removed?
	if it:isA("Weld") and it.Name == "SeatWeld" then-- if it's a weld and named like a seat weld
		byebye()-- time to shut down the car
		seat:SetNetworkOwnershipAuto()
	end
end)

function byebye()
	if engineBlock and carRunning then-- remember scripting level 3? that applies to below:
		if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Stopping") and engineBlock.Stopping:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Stopping:Play()-- where if it didn't do it like that, the above would error!
			wait(0.1)					-- because engineBlock.Stopping would not exist, so :IsA() is
		end								-- "not a valid member"
		if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Running") and engineBlock.Running:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Running:Stop()-- WE MUST NOT STOP
		end
		if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("WindSound") then
			engineBlock.WindSound:Stop()
		end
		if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Starting") and engineBlock.Starting:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Starting:Stop()-- THIS SCRIPT IS BREAK-PROOF >:U
		end
	end
	RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = 0--make sure that the car doesn't drive off without us
	carRunning = false
	lightsOn = false
	updatelights()--turn the lights off
	UpdateVehicle()--make sure the car stops now that it's off
end

RearLight = {}--all the brake lights. These contain one SpotLight that gets manipulated
RearBulb = {}--the brake lights that use a Surface GUI to light up
FrontLight = {}--same as the RearLight but for the front
FrontBulb = {}--same as the RearBulb but for the front
ReverseLight = {}--These light up when you reverse
ReverseBulb = {}--these also light up when you reverse: SurfaceGui style

for _, i in pairs (car:GetChildren()) do--populate the tables for ease of modularity.
	if i.Name == "RearBulb" then-- you can have any number of these items you want
		table.insert(RearBulb,i)-- and you don't have to change the script one bit!
	elseif i.Name == "RearLight" then
		table.insert(RearLight,i.SpotLight)
	elseif i.Name == "FrontLight" then
		table.insert(FrontLight,i.SpotLight)
	elseif i.Name == "ReverseLight" then
		table.insert(ReverseLight,i.SpotLight)
	elseif i.Name == "FrontBulb" then
		table.insert(FrontBulb,i)
	elseif i.Name == "ReverseBulb" then
		table.insert(ReverseBulb,i)
	elseif i:IsA("VehicleSeat") and i  ~= seat then
		i.ChildAdded:connect(function(it)-- something was added to our seat!
			if it:IsA("Weld") and it.Name == "SeatWeld" then-- is it the kind of thing players use to sit with?
				local plr = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(it.Part1.Parent)
				if plr then
					if car.SeatsLocked.Value and plr.Name ~= car.Owner.Value then
						wait(2/30)
						if it and it.Part1 and it.Part1.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
							it.Part1.Parent.Humanoid.Jump = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

function updatelights()-- this turns the lights on and off based on the lightsOn value
	for _,i in pairs (RearLight) do
		i.Enabled = lightsOn
	end
	for _,i in pairs (FrontLight) do
		i.Enabled = lightsOn
	end
	for _,i in pairs (FrontBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiStandard(i,lightsOn)
	end
	for _,g in pairs (RearBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiBrightness(g,lightsOn,seat.Throttle == 0)
	end
end

flippingDebounce = false-- a debounce is a simple way of preventing a function from
						-- being called multiple times while it's still running
function Flip()
	if not flippingDebounce then-- here's how it works: the function checks if the debounce is off
		flippingDebounce = true-- if it's on, then the function stops, but if it's off, set it to true
		local bodypos = Instance.new("BodyPosition",seat)-- so we get uninterupted function fun
		bodypos.maxForce = Vector3.new(100000,10000000,100000)
		bodypos.position = seat.Position + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
		local bodygyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro",seat)
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(bodypos, 3)-- this makes sure that the flippy bit
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(bodygyro, 3)-- deletes itself automatically
		wait(3)
		flippingDebounce = false-- when we are done with our fun, allow the function to work again
	end
end

function SurfaceGuiStandard(part,on)-- this makes it so the lights turn on or off
	for _,i in pairs (part:GetChildren()) do
		if i:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
			i.Enabled = on
		end
	end
end

function SurfaceGuiBrightness(part,on,bright)-- this is so the brake lights dim when we drive
	for _,i in pairs (part:GetChildren()) do
		if i:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
			i.Enabled = on
			if i:FindFirstChild("Frame") and i.Frame:IsA("Frame") then
				i.Frame.Transparency = bright and 0 or 0.5-- scripting level 4: go
			end-- for simple statements, you can have a boolean value decide between 2 values
			-- which is the same as writing	
			-- "if bright then
			--		i.Frame.Transparency = 0
			--	else
			--		i.Frame.Transparency = 0.5
			-- end"
			--but instead of "then" and "else", you use "and" and "or"
		end
	end
end

hornDebounce = false
function Honk()-- if you are reading this then congratulations! you win a prize!
	if hornDebounce then return end-- unfortunately, I could not think of a way
	if engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Horn") and engineBlock.Horn:IsA("Sound") then
		hornDebounce = true-- to allow people to be able to honk the horn for both PC and mobile
		engineBlock.horn:Play()	-- in an unobtrusive way. Sorry! :(
		print("beep beep") -- UristMcSparks, the guy in charge of the toolbox, doesn't want print statements
		wait(0.5)-- but since this is a secret, lets keep it between you and me, OK?
		engineBlock.horn:Stop()
		hornDebounce = false
	end
end

function UpdateVehicle()-- this is for the brake lights and speed and stuff like that
	if seat.Torque ~= 0 and type(seat.Torque) == "number" and RemoteControlled then
		RemoteControlled.Torque = math.abs(seat.Torque)
		seat.Torque = 0
	else
		seat.Torque = 0
	end
	if seat.Throttle == 1 and carRunning then-- if we're going forwards
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = forwardsSpeed-- give it forwards speed
	elseif seat.Throttle == -1 and carRunning then-- if we're going backwards
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = reverseSpeed-- give it backwards speed
	else
		RemoteControlled.MaxSpeed = 0 -- otherwise, give it 0 speed
	end
	if workspace.FilteringEnabled and RemoteControlled and RemoteControlled.Parent then--the idea from scripting level 2 applies to false as well
		RemoteControlled.Throttle = seat.Throttle-- that is, it also works for "~= false"
	end-- the reason this line exists is to prevent the server from doing something the client
	-- is already doing.
	for _,h in pairs (ReverseLight) do-- all these loops below are just updating the lights
		h.Enabled = lightsOn and seat.Throttle == -1
	end
	for _,i in pairs (ReverseBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiStandard(i,lightsOn and seat.Throttle == -1)
	end
	for _,j in pairs (RearLight) do
		j.Brightness = 4-math.abs(seat.Throttle)*3
	end
	for _,k in pairs (RearBulb) do
		SurfaceGuiBrightness(k,lightsOn,seat.Throttle == 0)
	end
end

seat.Changed:connect(UpdateVehicle)-- this is so that when someone drives the car
								   -- the brake and reverse lights change
								   -- and the speed of the car changes

while true do
	for i = 1, 60 do--60/30 == 2, 2 seconds between deciding if it's upside down
		wait()-- we want the steering and sfx to update as many times a second as we can
		if workspace.FilteringEnabled then
			if car:FindFirstChild("LeftMotor") then
				car.LeftMotor.DesiredAngle = seat.Steer*math.rad(40-RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/4)
			end
			if car:FindFirstChild("RightMotor") then
				car.RightMotor.DesiredAngle = seat.Steer*math.rad(40-RemoteControlled.Velocity.magnitude/4)
			end
		end
		-- below: make sure there's a sound to play
		if RemoteControlled and carRunning and engineBlock and engineBlock:FindFirstChild("Running") and engineBlock.Running:IsA("Sound") then
			engineBlock.Running.Pitch = CalculatePitch()-- and make sure that the car is on before trying
												-- to make sure the engine sounds good
			if engineBlock:FindFirstChild("WindSound") then
				engineBlock.WindSound.Pitch = CalculatePitch()
			end
		end
	end
	--         V this allows people to say if they want the car to flip over or not
	if allowFlipping and (seat.CFrame*CFrame.Angles(math.pi/2,0,0)).lookVector.Y < 0.2 then--am I upside down?
		Flip()--flip the car right side up
		
		-- as a fun experiment, see what happens if you replace "Flip()" with "Spawn(Flip)"
		-- and see what the difference is when you drive the car off the baseplate
		-- if you did it right, you'll notice that the engine sound changes differently!
	end

local PlayersService = Game:GetService('Players')

local Food = script.Parent

while not script:FindFirstChild("Creator") or not script.Creator.Value do wait() end
local creator = script.Creator.Value

while not script:FindFirstChild("Eating") do wait() end
local eatingScript = script.Eating

while not script:FindFirstChild("TextSpammer") do wait() end
local TextSpammer = script.TextSpammer


function AddGuis(player)
	local playerGui= player.PlayerGui
	if not playerGui then return end
	local screenGui = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
	screenGui.Parent = playerGui
	local nscript = TextSpammer:Clone()
	nscript.Parent = screenGui
	nscript.Disabled = false
end

-- When someone steps on the food, drop what they're holding and attach the food to them along with an eatingScript
-- (They must: Not be the creator, be alive, have a right arm, not be eating, and not be poisoned)
Food.Touched:connect(function(otherPart)
	if otherPart and otherPart.Parent and otherPart.Parent:IsA("Model") and not otherPart.Parent:FindFirstChild('ForceField') then
		local otherModel = otherPart.Parent
		local otherPlayer = PlayersService:GetPlayerFromCharacter(otherModel)
		if otherPlayer and otherPlayer ~= creator then return end
		local ra = otherModel:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or otherModel:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
		if otherModel:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and otherModel.Humanoid.Health > 0 and ra then
			local otherHumanoid = otherModel.Humanoid
			if not otherHumanoid:FindFirstChild(eatingScript.Name) and not otherHumanoid:FindFirstChild("Poisoned") then
				if Food:FindFirstChild("SelfDestruct") then Food.SelfDestruct:Destroy() end -- lol
				if Food:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro") then Food.BodyGyro:Destroy() end
				local children = otherModel:GetChildren()
				if otherPlayer then
					Spawn(function() AddGuis(otherPlayer) end)
				end
				Food.Parent = otherModel
				local weld = Instance.new("Weld", Game.JointsService)
				weld.Part0 = ra
				weld.Part1 = Food
				weld.C1 = CFrame.Angles(math.pi/2, 0, 0) + Vector3.new(0, 0, 1.4)
				local eatingScriptClone = eatingScript:Clone()
				Game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(eatingScriptClone, 30) -- In case non-player humanoids pick it up
				eatingScriptClone.Parent = otherHumanoid
				eatingScriptClone.Disabled = false
				--script:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end)

local COOLDOWN = 22 -- Seconds until another can be thrown
local TIME_TO_LIVE = 10 -- Seconds until each old one is destroyed
local THROW_SPEED = 100

-- Waits for parent.child to exist, then returns it
local function WaitForChild(parent, childName)
	assert(parent, "ERROR: WaitForChild: parent is nil")
	while not parent:FindFirstChild(childName) do parent.ChildAdded:wait() end
	return parent[childName]
end

local PlayersService = Game:GetService('Players')
local DebrisService = Game:GetService('Debris')

local Tool = script.Parent
local ToolHandle = Tool.Handle

local ThrowSound = WaitForChild(ToolHandle, 'Throw')

--[[
-- {PoisonApple, PoisonCake, PoisonPorkChop}
local BaseIdUrl = 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id='
local IconIds = {'87862872', '87863072', '87871704'}
local MeshIds = {'87862943', '87863104', '87863169'}
local TextureIds = {'87862921', '87863090', '87863148'}
local HandleSizes = {Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2), Vector3.new(1.08, 1.11, 1.14), Vector3.new(0.45, 1.82, 1.33)}
--]]

local MyModel = nil
local MyPlayer = nil

uses = 0

local function OnEquipped()
	MyModel = Tool.Parent
	MyPlayer = PlayersService:GetPlayerFromCharacter(MyModel)
end

local function OnActivated()
	if not MyModel or not MyModel:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or not Tool.Enabled then return end
	Tool.Enabled = false
	
	uses = uses + 1

	ThrowSound:Play()

	local handleClone = ToolHandle:Clone()
	DebrisService:AddItem(handleClone, 30)
	--handleClone.Velocity = (MyModel.Humanoid.TargetPoint - ToolHandle.CFrame.p).unit * THROW_SPEED
	handleClone.Name = 'Food'
	handleClone.Parent = Workspace
	handleClone.Transparency = 0
	handleClone.CanCollide = true
	Instance.new('BodyGyro', handleClone) -- Keeps it upright
	local selfDestructScript = handleClone:FindFirstChild('SelfDestruct')
	if selfDestructScript and selfDestructScript:FindFirstChild('TimeToLive') then
		selfDestructScript.TimeToLive.Value = TIME_TO_LIVE
		selfDestructScript.Disabled = false
	end
	local foodScript = handleClone:FindFirstChild('PoisonedFood')
	if foodScript then
		local creator = Instance.new('ObjectValue', foodScript)
		creator.Name = 'Creator'
		creator.Value = MyPlayer
		foodScript.Disabled = false
	end
	handleClone.CFrame = Tool.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame
	
	if uses >= 3 then
		Tool:Destroy()
	end

	--[[ Load new item (while we wait)
	local index = math.random(3)
	ToolHandle.Mesh.MeshId = BaseIdUrl .. MeshIds[index]
	ToolHandle.Mesh.TextureId = BaseIdUrl .. TextureIds[index]
	ToolHandle.Size = HandleSizes[index] -- This breaks the weld
	MyWeld.Parent = Game.JointsService -- Fix the weld
	Tool.TextureId = BaseIdUrl .. IconIds[index] -- Change the tool icon
	--]]

	wait(COOLDOWN)

	Tool.Enabled = true
	
end

local function OnUnequipped()
	ToolHandle.Transparency = 0
end

Tool.Equipped:connect(OnEquipped)
Tool.Activated:connect(OnActivated)
Tool.Unequipped:connect(OnUnequipped)
